Good, I just wanted my suspicions confirmed. Betrayal comes fast these days.
After their time with the dragon, Nia and Andy made sure they had everything they
wanted to take with them on their long trip to the rocky region. They had been there before, so
they had a feeling of what it was like to be there. They had always felt small among the large
spikes and awkward against the strange rock shapes.
They were able to start their journey after the dragon had given them a mirror, in order
for them to talk with the ice dragon, and in return, the ice dragon asked for their other mirror.
They then could communicate with both dragons, and the dragons could communicate too.
Their trip had started and they were back on the road. They loathed a bed because they
had not slept on the ground for a while. Consequently, they had some issues in the morning with
their back. They had trouble sleeping as well. They had enough strength still to walk, so walk
they did. The waves of the ice continued, but this time, it was not in their favor. They had to
walk all the way to the edge of the snow region so that they could travel easier. Their food was
replenished, so they did not worry about food like they had on the way there, but if they were
going to traverse difficult terrain, they would need extra energy for the extra exertion.
The second day on the road, Nia woke up to a good breakfast that Andy had made
especially for her, because he knew she liked it, even though it was simple. She was grateful for
the gesture, and she knew he cared for her and would not let anything happen to her.
The land, as they walked that day calmed and they thought that it was very beautiful.
They would not have thought otherwise, unless it looked like a dump. The ice shone in the sun
and it was very wonderful to them. They ended up staring at the sight for a few hours. As the
sun set, the sight was reflected in the mirror-like ice. Then they suddenly thought that they
would have all the help they would need. There would be more merry things ahead of them, it
was not all bad. Others would await them and be willing to help.
When they set off the next day, they were calmed and they knew that they could get out
of the tundra. Even as they walked, they noticed that the tundra was changing. It was shedding
its ice in some places and breaking revealing parts that resembled what the land looked like
before. There were puddles everywhere and they had to avoid them in order to not be wet and so
their feet could stay dry. If they got wet they would be very unhealthy and not fit for walking
more.
The ground was not quite green in the spots that were melted, but they would be very
soon as spring was approaching. They had been in the palace for longer than they had now
realized, and it was now very close to becoming spring. The days seemed short even though they
were getting longer. Spring months came very quickly in the land because the winter months
lasted for only one or two months. Since the winter months came early, the spring season also
seemed to shift.
They were still able to make better progress than they were in the snow and they
managed to get out of the tundra very quickly. During the days that they were in the tundra, they
had many storms, but this time, they were rain storms that drenched them to their skin.
When they got to the mountains, instead of going through them again, they traveled
along the side of them and down. By doing this, they would not have to face the brutality of the
mountains and at the same time moving in the right direction. They needed to get to the rock
region, so they were going the right direction. They needed to also find out about the rumor of
the man who burnt down their town. They needed to ask about that man and find where he was
so they could get revenge. Nia was sure that Andy would want revenge too, so they had a silent
agreement to find the man.
On the day that they were three days from the palace, they had been walking along the
mountains for one day. The mountains did not seem so big as they had when they had first come
through them. The mountains did not throw snow at them, so they were comfortable that day to
calm down.
They were having their lunch and talking about how they would go about finding the
man that had started the fire. They thought they might not even go to the rock region, but try to
go wherever the man had gone. If they could find him, they could help free the region as well as
getting their revenge. There was a town along the mountains, so they planned to stop at it and
ask about the rumor. They would be at the town in about a day and they did not want to attract
unwanted attention. Andy shared an idea with Nia about how they would get the information
they needed. He wanted them to go to the inns first and ask, then go to the houses.
Nia did not think that going to the houses was a good idea, but she also knew that you
often had to dig for information if you wanted the knowledge. She would do it even if she was
not for it. She trusted Andy to do the critical thinking, even she thought that he would be
smarter because of all the time he had to think out in the wilderness.
When they got to the town the next day, they went about doing their plan as well as
collecting more food and purchasing it with money they had gotten from the dragon as a gift.
They were able to find a good deal about the man that they were looking for. The amount
of information the people had in the inns alone was a great quantity and it was quite unexpected
for both of them. They rented a room for their stay, and they stayed there for one night so they
could sleep in an actual bed and have the comfort of it.
They were on their way early in the morning without a word and they did not bother to
check out of the inn, which was apparently required in this one. They did not get any extra
disturbance because of it, but they thought it was a strange thing that they had to check out.
They made good progress by setting off early and they were very eager to be on the road.
They saw many people traveling on the roads and they liked seeing people all the time because
they did not have to look at the ground all the time. Even though the scenery changed, they
needed something more, which is why it was good to see the other people, people that were not
engaged in a war.
Nia had wanted to go back to the life she had had before because of the comfort, but she
was coming for Andy, and she also had a desire to save the land. For what would there be to go
back to if they did not save the land. They had so many people counting on them to save the
land, and many of them had probably heard about the prophecy. The suffering that would take
place if they did not save the land would be devastating, there might not even be someone left
for them to save if they did not act.
When she thought about this, she remembered who she had. She had Andy, the dragons,
the others fighting for the same cause. There were so many that would be willing to help her,
she just had to seek for them. She had to have the courage to ask. Asking was such a small thing,
but it could bring things to happen that are so large that no one could comprehend them. There
was help.
The information they learned about the man was very repetitive, but they managed to
flesh out a few details out of every explanation. Many of the people were wary of the town and
maybe even afraid of Alabak. Nia thought that was irrational and that people should not assume
that since the people from the town were there, that they were all just criminals. They had
gotten the information they needed, and apparently, the man from the town was in a different
town at the edge of the desert region.
That was their destination. Even though they were going to go to the rock region, they
decided that it would be more efficient to go to the desert and try and find the man. Nia
wondered who would go to the desert to hide. They would probably have the hardest time
surviving them. They were reckless enough to go into the desert after that man though, and they
knew they were going to face extra challenges such as the dragon and getting to find him. They
also had to fight the fire that they knew was going to be harder to subdue than the last one.
12: Fighting Your Own Conscience
Andy had been a little low or more cautious than he usually was because he had been
worried what Nia would think if they found Liam. She would never expect that it was him. If
they found him there in the dry place, they would most definitely have no question that he was
the traitor.
Now they just had to get there if they were going to accomplish anything. They had to go
quickly, they knew that they were on a time limit against the fading of the regions. They were
eager to be on the road, and their desire to save the land was increasing so that it was something
they genuinely wanted to do.
When they got back onto the road, they had a full inventory of what they needed and
they were fully prepared for anything that they would need. Andy was very eager to be on the
road, partly because of his vengeful lust, also because he too felt urgent to save the land from
torment.
They made good progress the first day, and they also felt as if they were going to do
something that would be remembered for ages after them. They were able to go with pride into
what they were doing. With this pride, they would be able to save the land from torment and
also be able to find the courage to do more, even if that meant the death of them. They both
were open to talk to each other more, because they both had common goals and interests. They
had also not talked openly for a while.
Andy thought that Nia also might recognize what he was trying to hold back too, so he
wanted her to feel like she was not being left out. They had to become closer together if they
were to defeat the enemy, and that could not happen without any communication. With the
mindset, Andy was very willing to talk about what awaited them and maybe what they were to
do in the future. If all went well and they were able to defeat the enemy, what would they do?
Andy could not think of what they would do, for their town had been burned down and they had
no place to call home.
Something familiar returned to the front of his mind, pushed by something also familiar.
Leave it till it comes, tomorrow is not here yet, leave it. Andy knew then that the words were similar
to the words of Eradris. He was grateful for the bird, and he knew that he should listen to the
advice. He then thought about what the bird would do.
They encountered something strange on their way to the desert though, something that
disturbed them and made them cautious. There were hundreds of lights that moved as they
approached, down in a valley as the sun adjourned. The lights appeared to be like an army was
on the move. The army, at least from their distance, seemed like it was shorter than a man’s
height, but still walked on two legs.
There were so many of them, Andy could not think of what they could be. They then
looked closer and Eradris said, They are goblins, you ought to go to sleep though, if something comes
near you, then I will warn you. Also, do not have a fire tonight.
They backed away, back into the forest that they had been traveling in. The forest was
grown out of oak trees and they did not see any other tree besides oak, but they did see many
plants throughout the forest. The plants were mostly familiar to Andy, all except a few. As they
set up the camp site, without a fire, they collected some of the edible plants and were able to fill
their stomachs with berries and some leaves from a nettle bush. They also had some bread with
their meal to fill them. They had gotten plenty of money to buy materials from the ice dragon.
There was no shortage, every time they reached into the pouch that they were given, they found
more coins.
They went to bed that night, anxious of the goblins and their number, though they could
sleep fine, they did not get to sleep until late in the night. They slept well for the time that they
slept, though they did not sleep very long. They were woken up because of something, they did
not know what. They were not aware enough to tell if it was a branch cracking underneath a
foot, or just animal noises. They had to be aware of what they were going to do if something did
happen. They suddenly received the direction that they needed from Eradris.
You need to go north, behind you, and you will find that you will be able to escape fast enough,
or at least, you will be able to find shelter. Go to the north, quickly, help will be there, just be willing.
They did as he said and ran north, if there was going to be help, they wanted it. A force
as big as the goblins’ they had seen last night could easily take over a small village, though that
did not seem what it would be. They went to the north and they found a path, when they got to
the path, they were told to follow it by Eradris.
As they followed the path, they were ready to fight, so they were very tense and not
stable. They were relieved that they were on the path so that they might see other people, as well
as maybe find somewhere they could be safe. The benefit of being on the path was that, and they
could also possibly find someone traveling just on the road. That would be a help, but they did
not see anyone at the time being. They kept going on the path, even though they were not sure if
they would reach a town or not.
They thought they saw something on the road ahead of them, so they ran, however, at
the same time, they heard many tromping feet behind them. They ran to the person, even
though they knew it would not be enough for them to be saved. They still felt somewhat safe
because there was another person. They went towards the person, and reached them soon. Out
of the trees that they had come out of came the tromping goblins that they had seen previously.
Had the goblins seen them in the dark, or did they just stumble into them?
Whatever the case, the person that they had seen was riding a horse, and when they got
close enough, the horse spooked. The horse would not have been frightened by just the two of
them, but it probably saw the hoard that chased after them. Andy was able to see the face of the
horseman, and what he saw was not what he either expected, nor what he was prepared for at
the time.
The face that Andy saw was not good, but it was a person. The face was of his
acquaintance, Liam. He did not know what to think, though he was afraid of what Nia would
think. If she got too comfortable with him before she knew the truth about him, then it could be
a bad thing for both of them. They also could be halted by this occurrence. Andy’s biggest
wonder was how he got there and what he was doing in the meantime. Andy wondered if Liam
had been doing things for the desert kingdom, or even worse, the fire.
“You! You are alive?” He shouted.
“We have not the time! Go!” Andy shouted.
Liam looked up and then took off in the other direction. He was off in a flash, “There is a
town you could refuge in, follow quick!” he shouted as he flew off back down the way he had
come.
Andy followed and Nia was close behind. Andy had to run like the wind so he could
escape, and Nia could run fast enough to keep up with him. They did not know how long they
had to run, but they knew that they would have to keep running, or give up. As they ran, they
were able to get a far distance, but the goblin forces were gaining ground. They could not keep
running like this, for the goblins were probably trained and had great endurance.
When they were about to be overrun, their hearts were pounding and pumping blood all
throughout their body quickly. They were tired, and they would be caught, something came to
his mind, it was from Liam.
Here, take my strength. Liam was awed as he felt Andy’s mind, and Andy could feel it.
Liam then filtered a burst of magic through Andy. It was a word. Andy did not know what it
was, but he did know that they had not been overcome by the goblins. He wondered if the word
was supposed to help with the hindrance of the goblins. He knew though that they were close to
the town, for he could see a little light. The light was very bright, but Andy knew that the light
would be the town. For the connection between Liam and Andy grew a little stronger as they
ran.
In the distance, they could also see the growing light of the sun. The sun was behind
them, but he knew that the brightening sky was the sign of dawn. Andy had a smile brought to
his face as he looked back at the goblins. He saw that they were falling behind, or at least losing
ground. The goblins were losing their strength as the day grew too bright. Andy was delighted
to see that they would be triumphant by the spell, or the great hindrance that Liam had given.
When they reached the town, the goblins were barely in sight, and dispersing. They had
many questions to ask Liam, whether they be good or bad questions. He had much answering to
do.
He was waiting for them outside of the town walls, and he was walking towards them as
they came into his view. The sun was not up, but the light was spraying over the mountains to
the east. The mountains were not as large as the ones in the tundra, but they were fair sized.
When they met together, Liam said, “Come, you will be pleased, I have a few things that you
might need.”
He did not say anything about the awe that he had when he had contacted Andy, but
Andy suspected that he would ask eventually. He was ready to answer, so he was able to get the
best answers and ask the best questions. Andy had learned something when talking to people,
efficient talking was the key to getting them to say what you wanted them to say.
Communicating to people in general was very important. If you did not communicate to people,
things could go terribly wrong.
Liam led them into the town and into the inn that was first seen in it. There was a place
that he had, and he was just going to get a few things from a town not far away when he left.
Andy thought that it could be very much a lie and that he could just be saying it to divert
attention.
He got some of the stuff, which turned out to be a few scraps of food as well as some
blankets. He asked, “What things do you have there in those packs?”
“We have plenty of food, blankets, and money.” Nia replied, though not revealing
anything else. Andy was surprised she did not, they did not want to say anything else.
Andy then asked, “What have you been doing?”
“I thought I would go to Alabak, then try to find work there and hopefully something to
do off in the world. I wanted to see what was here. After the storm and fire, I woke up after
being hit by a fiery log, luckily surviving. I was weak, but able to find a horse that had run off. I
lost my way multiple times , and it took a while to find sometimes after I lost it. I then made my
way to Alabak in hopes of finding you, but I did not, so then I came here, because it might have
been another place I could find people fleeing from the village.”
“When I found no one, I went to this town and found a few of the villagers but could not
go back because of the temperature that closed in. When, finally, I was able to be on the move
again, I started doing work here to earn money. Oh, and not long ago, I heard a rumor that
somebody in the village, our village, was the one who started the fire. But, what have you guys
been doing as of the past months?”
“Well, we have had a very interesting time looking for the others. We knew that we could
not linger, neither did we want to go, but we figured we ought to move on after the villagers on
foot got here. We went to the forest to see what was there, then after that…” Andy paused in his
talking. “Well, when you reached my mind, what did you see? Please be fully honest.”
“I only felt something, I felt some great thing that I hadn;t felt before. It felt as if you had
found something extraordinary. So then I used the potential as well as my energy to find a spell
that would hinder the goblins. I found that I can do a few simple things with magic, but I can
never get huge things. I was only able to say the word that I knew and have you say it before you
were overrun.”
“Well, what is my potential, what did you mean?”
I felt like there was something inside of you, waiting to come out. It was something that
you hadn’t learned yet, or something that was about to burst out of you.”
“Well.” Andy did not say more, but he did go into Liam’s mind. Eradris recognized what
he was doing and joined him so that he could hide their presence in his mind. They went
unobserved by Liam, but Eradris fed more questions into Andy so that he could not appear
suspicious of something. They found nothing that was a sign of his guilt. They thought they
would find something that would give them clues about him and evidence of the burning down
of the town. They went through his mind not finding anything until they found a string of
events that followed his way all the way back a year, when nothing had happened.
Liam had been in Alabak so he could retrieve some supplies for a smith in their small
village. When he had gotten the tools, he had seen some strange things in Alabak and wanted to
get away from them. He went quickly, but felt as if something was amiss. He later went back to
the town and something seemed to be stewing there, something that was about to break. He got
what he needed, returned to the town, and a few months later the village had burnt down
because of an unusual storm. His mind kept returning to the strange feeling of something
brewing when he thought of the occurrence of the town. They found no other things concerning
the town, and nothing else that would signify his treachery. He turned out to be innocent.
“…So then we arrived here.” Andy finished a question, more trusting in Liam now.
“Now what do you plan to do, where do you plan to go?”
“Well, if we told you, you would not believe us, but we are going to find a dragon in the
desert kingdom. We were surprised by the goblins, and now we plan to go there, to the desert.”
“What is in the desert?”
“In every region, there is a dragon, we have met two of them and were able to save both
of them from a fire that festered in the form of a specific thing, or weakness of the region.
Dragons formed to hinder the fires, but they are only able to hinder it. If but one land succeeds
in finding the weakness to its region, then the power will grow, and it will be much easier for
the darkness to take over. We have been saving the land.”
Liam pursed his lips, “Well, that is quite the story. I have many questions, but if the
desert is under attack by something evil, we better help go save it, plus, it looks like you could
use another companion.”
“Yes, that would be welcome.” Andy trusted Liam to the former extent he had before, but
he was still leery of what the power he had was. Andy needed answers, and that was what there
was not enough of. Though, Liam was right, they needed to be off. They could still make some
good time if they started soon.
There were a few things they had to attend to before they left. Liam had to gather his
materials, and Andy and Nia would need a horse to ride if they were going to keep up. They had
enough money for two horses, so they bought two good looking horses that they could ride and
that seemed strong.
They were soon on their way to their desert destination, and they traveled with plenty of
food and some extra water this time. They traveled along, and Liam always had another question
for them to answer, and another answer always came, for what else were they going to do? Andy
actually enjoyed the explanations he gave, they seemed to help clear his understanding of them
too.
They continued through the day, stopping to cook a meal between the day. The meals
were small, and did not have a lot of flavor until Liam pulled out a small box of black pepper,
and a small bag of salt. The meals were better from then on and they were able to have a better
time of things. They told him about things as they ate, as they were on the road, and as they laid
down to sleep. There was so much to tell, and so much to do.
It took a while for them to reach the desert border, but they knew that they would be
able to get past the desert quite easily, that is, if nothing went wrong. They had never had much
information about the desert, but they always knew about the things that were possible there.
They could possibly run into sinkholes and the always scorching climate, but they could already
feel that coming on. If they were to make the best time though, they had no time to waste. The
things that were needed could be the things that they had forgotten. After all, they had not gone
to the rock region like was originally planned. That region was what provisions they had been
given to traverse. Now that they were crossing a desert, they would be more susceptible, as well
as the things that could have spread about them.
Andy often wondered as they traveled, what things that he was capable of. Liam had a
little magical power, and he could see something about him, but Andy could not see it in his
own self. They had encountered a river prior and recent to their arrival into the desert, and they
had been able to get the water they would need, but then they wondered about their horses. How
would their thirst be quenched in the desert of nothing but sand. After a short while of traveling
in the land, an idea occurred to Andy and he voiced it when they set up a camp for the night.
“For the problem with the horses, we could try to find a well, some of the wells could be
deep enough for us to retrieve water. If we can find one, we could water the horses sufficiently.”
“There will probably be fierce competition if we tried to take water from the well, usually
there will be someone to guard them. It would not be good for our reputation if we have
dealings with the lord of the city,” came the reply, “he does not take well to people that trespass
on his land and steal his provisions.”
“Well, we could always ask, plus, I do not think that we will be meeting the king, we
want to be as unobtrusive as possible.”
Liam only nodded his head and said, “Well alright.”
Their horses would be tired if they did not have water, and they probably would not be
able to control them as well if they were unwilling. They traveled in the land for a little while the
next day, when they saw a small village not too far off and went to ask if they would be open to
letting them drink as well as their horses. Liam muttered something and they looked confused
at why they would ask, but then one of the men in front said, “Well, you do have horses, and you
probably need to see the lord of the land, so you may.”
They made sure to thank them, and then proceeded to take what they needed for
themselves and their horses. Once they were full, and their animals had been watered, they
proceeded. Andy had made sure to tell the animals that he had been grateful for what they had
done for him, but they seemed to be ignorant of his calls the first few times. Andy wondered if it
was because they were tired. Andy knew that they would be able to find another town, because
of a view from Eradris, as well as some information from the villagers in the town.
When they knew their heading, they were able to set off without a single word but the
thanks they had left with the people.
When they were out of the earshot of the people, Andy looked over at Liam who was
content as could be and asked, “How did you make them give us the water? We might be able to
use that again, and you could be able to teach it to me. Do you think I could learn that?”
“I have no doubt that you could learn it, as for the time, it would not be good, though I
could try if you wish, to teach it to you.”
“Yes, it would be a most helpful skill.”
Alright, I can teach you on the road, though it will be difficult. So, I want you to reach
into my mind and look at my thoughts, and then think what you want me to think. It is as simple
as that.”
Andy reached into the mind of Liam and then thought what he wanted him to think, he
felt his mind become part of his mind and he told Liam what to think. Andy was glad that he
trusted him enough to let him into his mind. Andy thought now that he had just been overly
cautious and that he had misjudged Liam. When he withdrew from the mind of Liam, he was
able to tell him that it worked the first time.
“Good, now try to reach in and fight against my mind. I will not be mad at you if you fail,
or mad if you win, it is just, I have probably had more practice. If you get into the mind and it is
protected by something, you will have to fight their mind. Never go against someone that you
don’t know or that you cannot surpass. Go ahead, try.”
As Andy reached into the mind of Liam once more, he repeated the process, but he felt
the resistance that Leam had said would be present. It felt small, then it grew as Andy tried to
push past it. He was blocked from seeing the things that he had before, and he then increased
his efforts. Andy pushed at the mind of Liam, then something occurred to him, if he could
distract him, then he could overtake him. He came up with an image of his sword in his mind
and stabbed at the obstacle. A twinge of pain inside got to him and he felt the mental block
recede a little. Andy used the momentum to drive his own force forward from all around and the
block gave way, and Andy once more had access to Liam's mind.
A gasp escaped Liam as Andy withdrew from his mind, “You defeated my defenses. I did
not think of using something like that to get to someone. I wish I could have used that before.
Maybe you should try with Nia. If we could all do it, then we could be a powerful team.”
“Do you want to?” Andy asked Nia.
“Well, you won’t hurt me will you,” It was not a question. “Yes.” she said with her usual
smile.
Liam once again gave the instructions, and Nia tried to reach into Andy’s mind. Andy let
his defenses fall and he let her feel around. He could feel her amazement and he was glad she
had learned how to perform the first item. Andy then instructed her how he had done it to do it
the same way with him.
She again went into his mind and used something that had a will of its own to overtake
him. She used the minds of all the animals she had felt to help her. The combined force was too
much for Andy to withstand, so he gave in.
“Well, I think we should both take lessons from her.” Andy said, looking at Liam and
raising his eyebrows.
“What did she do?”
“Maybe I’ll let you find that out.”
“Alright, let's see.” Liam let her reach to his mind and Andy could feel the animals'
minds combining within her mind as she easily broke the defenses of Liam's mind very quickly
and she was able to pour her thoughts to him and make him think what she wanted him to.
When she was through, and withdrew from his mind, he was able to tell that she was
pleased by what she was able to do. They kept practicing throughout the day, and they got very
drastic in their attempts to overcome each other. Andy wondered if they could call upon the
minds of the dragons that they had met to help them if they ever got into a situation where they
had to fight for their mind.
They also wondered together if you could defend your mind against non physical things
like hallucination or other things. It was a prospect he could not resist, so when he got to the
desert when it would be hot, he would try it.
Andy was happy as they went along because of the things that he was able to do and he
was able to see many things. He was also able to talk to another whom may be able to help them,
then something occurred to Andy that made him laugh at the fact that he had not given it heed
before.
“Do you plan on coming with us? Or do you have to do something in the desert
kingdom?”
“I do not have anything better to do, so I think that I will come with you so I can help in
any way that I can and maybe learn more, as you two have. I always like to find out things that
are going on in the world.”
“Well then, welcome to this little traveling circus. We perform every other week for all
who want to come.” Andy laughed and Nia just shook her head and looked at the saddle. While
she just looked down, Liam had a grin on his face and he was leaning back in his saddle.
It just fueled Andy to laugh more and it just turned into a paradox that continued to run
as well as eventually triggered Nia to laugh at their foolishness.That only made them laugh
harder and they went off the path a little so they had to redirect their horses back onto the path.
They were jerked back to their senses as that happened and they did not allow themselves to go
on another laughing tangent. They had no idea why it was so funny that they laughed for so
long, but they enjoyed it.
13: Tewelors and Wincher Wales
They were well into the desert by the end of the next day, and they were able to test
Andy’s theory about the mind altering. When he tried to reach into his own mind, he told
himself that everything would be alright even if he was not entirely sure he would be. He
reached in and cleared his mind of everything but the thought. He focused on that thought and
he could feel the cold of the desert night start to fade away. Was it his altering of his mind, or
was it just raw brain power?
Andy reached into Nia’s mind and she let him take over her thoughts and convince her
that she was alright. He, as he withdrew from her mind, was already starting to taste the effects
of his alteration.
He did the same with Liam and then took a moment to gaze at the landscape for a while.
The land did not seem to have any variety and the land was flat as could be thought. There was
still a path, though, so they followed where it led so they could get to the kingdom and possibly
try to find a lead on where the fire might be.
The next day, they were glad to see that the alteration had been there, though they
suspected it would change as they went along. Andy was eager to be moving, for he started to
get a thought in his mind that made him uncomfortable. He voiced his thoughts to the group,
and they said that they felt it too. They had to be cautious once again, and they were able to
travel without incident for the first few days.
Andy thought that they would be fine for those days, and he was right, it was the fading
clearness in his mind that he thought might be causing his caution. Andy had been able to do
many things over the time of those few short days, though he also wondered what was causing
the wariness. It disturbed him, and he did not know what he thought of it since both of the
others felt it too.
When on their third day into the desert, they encountered something unusual, they were
not able to define what it was. It was as if confusion had suddenly struck them, and they did not
know why they were going into the desert. They continued, only knowing that they had a reason
and that it might be their only course of action. If they were going to do something they might
as well do what they had been, even though it might not have been sensible. Andy, however,
knew that something was playing with their minds, but every time he thought about it,
something seemed to brush its significance away.
That night, they said nothing but did as they had always done in the night time as they
traveled. They set up their camp, got ready for bed, but then they seemed distracted, not able to
go to bed. As they listened to the silence enveloping them, they heard something, and they all
looked towards it like they were programmed to do it. There seemed to be a voice somewhere in
the distance that sang.
They were entranced by it, and they started walking over to the sound. Suddenly, they
encountered a sand dune, the only one they had seen in the entire desert kingdom. For some
reason the dune looked funny to them, and they started losing their ability to act for themselves.
The falcon also seemed to be caught in the trance. Andy was almost to the dune, when a light
appeared over it and got brighter, he did not know if he should call it light though.
The light was not bright, rather it was the darkest thing that he had ever seen. A ball of
light, or black light appeared over the dune and it sang. It sang a high pitched tune and it
sounded like its voice was a rusty sheet of metal scraping on stone and it was shrill. Andy could
not turn away, none of them could.
The rusty voices of the light sang and they were almost to them when they saw the six
inch teeth that they had… and the menacing purple eyes. Its eyes were pure purple not the
smallest amount of a different color resided within his eyes.
The creature itself apparently was not enough, so more came. More of the waling
creatures with their voices and eyes. Some of their eyes were red though, and some were like
fire. The creatures were not only singing, but seemed to be staring at them, staring deep.
Then the trance seemed to break, but they could not move, they could not. When they
tried to move a body part, they felt the strain of doing the movement, but they did not budge.
The creatures stared at them, and Andy was afraid, though if he was able to think for himself he
would be running in the opposite direction. The winchers pushed forward, and something broke
the silence in Andy’s mind. It was a scream, but he knew it was not from Liam or Nia. In Andy's
mind, a flood of memories plowed through all of his other senses, uncontrolled and he knew
that it would stop as soon as the creatures regained possession of their minds. As he sunk into
the flow of the memories and let himself be taken by them. He was grateful for the small rest
that he had been given.
Through his mind rushed a time when he had been in the town, back when it had not
burned down. A time where he had spoken to an animal when he was younger and still learning
a lot about the animals. A time when he had stabbed his foot with a rusty nail and it had to be
treated. The memories grew more frequent, and Andy became unaware of what happened in
them. Then finally it slowed, and a different memory came to his mind, one that was not his
own.
It was of someone standing in what seemed to be a treasure room, mixed with a dungeon. The
dungeon was dimly lit and the room seemed to fade into the corners of the view. Two men came down the
stairs that were in front of the man, and the man prepared for what they had to ask him. He had to
protect the artifact no matter what. If he let its power loose, it would rule death over the land and spread
a fire of torment, a fire of despair and darkness. He knew that he was powerful and could stand up to
anything that anyone could throw at him. The men asked, as he expected, but they then started to gain in
the intensity of their desire. Their voices started to rage and throw insults at the man. The man kept
himself composed and was able to only answer the questions that they had with a simple, repetitive, “No.”
The ‘No’ did not sit too well with them and they knew they could not beat the man, so they were
literally helpless and their pleas were useless. The man did not have to sleep or eat if someone had
forgotten to give him the time he needed, though he did get rest. The men were at their height, and the
man had heard this before from them, though what they did next broke every rule placed down by those
who first discovered magic four millennia ago. Their rage increased as the man had not seen before, and
suddenly a darkness entered the room that was very heavy and pressed in on them, though the men did
not seem to notice.
The men suddenly stopped though feeling the power of the darkness they had summoned. They
stood still feeling the power, then simultaneously, they looked at each other.
Darkness seemed to radiate from his words as he commanded the other, “Fin, conjure up some
Wincher Wales,” the evil smile only made the matters worse, and they both smiled their evil smirks. Even
he, the most powerful wizard in the land, or maybe the world, could not stand up to those. They could
tear him apart or even worse, turn his will against him. He wished that he would not be consumed by the
darkness too and used for horrible purposes.
He cowered in the corner, trying to think of a spell to prevent himself from being used as well as
one that could keep anyone out of the room he guarded. He needed to think fast before they finished the
incantation. He could not stop them, for a will like that, to conjure a Wincher Wale or more than that
meant you did not have a will, or a mind of your own. He could not break their concentration, nor could
he break their minds. He fumbled with words that would accomplish his will before it was too late, but
then, a blinding light filled the room. The Wincher Wales that appeared approached the man that
cowered in the corner, their singing was loud and he could not hold against it for long. They would soon
break him.
They were about to succeed, but then suddenly, words appeared in his mind that allowed him to
accomplish the goal. He said them, and just in time, for his mind broke. They got in, but they hadn’t. The
wizard, out of pure chance had held out to the singing so long that he had no strength to think of what he
had said. He had turned himself into something in the phrase, as well as accomplishing what he wanted.
He had managed even to thwart the Wincher Wales, and he flew away on silent, graceful wings through
the dungeon.
As the dream, memory, or whatever it was ended, Andy felt something fighting against
the Wincher Wales that had ensnared Andy’s mind. He could not join the fight, though, he could
only listen to the mental battle. He listened until one side started to overpower the other side,
He could catch glimpses of the mind of Nia, Liam, and the mind of Eradris. Eradris was fighting
like crazy and with more vigor than Andy had ever seen. He was able to join the fight when he
broke free with the help from his friends and they overpowered the Wincher Wales. The first
one that they had seen exploded as they broke its mind but the others escaped from them only to
maybe come back another time.
They all withdrew from the explosion and let out a sigh. The Wincher Wales were gone,
had they actually overpowered them? If what Andy saw in the dream was true, then they had
overpowered one of them and so ended whoever’s curse it was that had been a powerful wizard.
Andy wondered how long the man had been alive or how long he had been fighting to get free.
Andy then wondered what the wizard would be like if Andy got the chance to meet him.
What did the wizard look like now, and where was the dungeon that he cast a spell upon to

things, and food to survive and especially water. As they walked over to the tents, they had to be
cautious not to be caught unaware by something else that might want to ensnare their attention.
They stayed in the camp for a while talking but then headed into their tent so they could

were all grateful for his offer and they could not deny it, so they let him, he said he could not go
to sleep anyway.
They slept without disturbance for the remainder of the night and were able to think

the vision appeared to the others.
When Andy thought about the dream-like vision, he got confused about it more and

flowed and flowed as he slept, and he could not get any real rest. Then, as dreams and thoughts
slowed again, he gained rest.
When they woke up in the morning, another thing had come to stand at their front door.
There was a wandering man outside and the man did not seem to take heed of them. He seemed
as if he was wandering towards the Wincher Wales, but he was not confused, he could speak, but
he was not able to know where he was going.
The man was not shaken or crazy, but he was only confused. They walked up to the man
and told him that he did not have to wander, but the man only looked at him blankly and started
to reach. Andy and the others backed away, but they could not take a risk and let the man touch
them.
The scenery had changed throughout the night, and there was now a dune to the left of
the tent. They were curious about it, and they had to go in that direction anyway, but they did
not want the man to touch them so they packed up their camp and shook off the dirt that
accumulated on some of the provisions. They were glad for the tent, or they would have been
sandy in the morning.
After they had gotten to the dune and were fully packed up, they wanted to see what was

wandering people, men, women, children, and they all had a shocked or confused expression.
The man behind them moved slowly like the rest of the people, but they felt sad for them. They
had not the will to do anything differently.
“Should we try to reach to one of their minds?” Liam asked, looking them both in the

It would not be wise. Came Eradris.
“What was that, the voice? Did you hear it?” Liam asked.
“Ah, yes. That is a wise bird who is named Eradris. He was once human, but we must not
stay here.” With that, Andy walked at an angle to the creatures, as to avoid them.
Both of them followed, and they walked around the people wandering helplessly, just in

signifies to them where we are? Andy thought, though his steps slowed to watch the man.
Go, we must not linger, but do not run, for it will draw more attention.
Andy renewed his steps’ speed and started off for the day faster and more motivated than
he had been that morning. Though he was not able to see very well, Andy thought he saw the

the heat to fool his senses.
Nothing happened as they traveled that day, but when night approached, Andy got the
feeling that they were being watched by something. It was not the same feeling he had had other
times, like in the rock region, it was as if many eyes ground into his back, and even his front. He
did not like the feeling, and it only grew as the sun started to set.
They set up their camp with a small fire that night, because they all had felt the
uneasiness. When Andy got up to get a drink of water from his bag, he saw the sand stir and
rise. He stepped back into the brightest area that the fire lit.
He went to wake the others and at least make it so they were aware of the goings on.
Andy waited to see what would emerge out of the ground. Out of the ground came a black

from the figures, and they moaned long, deep, and eerie.
Not one, but many started pushing out of the ground. Then heads came out, then

The figures started to surround them, but they could not stay there, they also could not

original direction to the west, so they went toward the north.
If they were going, they would have to leave quickly and quick is how they left. They had
to be quick and careful as they could be, though they also had to avoid the begging calls of the
shadows. As they were looking for the ways that they could escape from the shadows, they were

They were safe for a little while, though the creatures started coming out of the ground

took rests. The creatures seemed to learn and become faster in their movement.
They may not be able to last the night. If they could, what would happen to the shadows?
They had come out when the sun had set, and as the night was progressing, they had gotten
faster and more determined. They had to get as far away from the shadows as they could before
the night reached its peak. If they could do that, then they might have a chance of outrunning
the shadows and being able to make it to tomorrow. If they could not do so, they would have to
give up their lives. If the speed of the shadows increased their speed at the rate they had been,
they would be able to overrun the group in less than an hour. They would gain their full speed
likely in the middle of the night when they thrived the best.
When they were in the middle of another rest, they took the time for a short
conversation, though they did not want to risk too much time for the creatures to climb out of

endless race, they would have to be faster than the monsters. They were able to agree that they

would be able to scout the area so they could find it easier.
Make sure that you stay connected with me though. If you need my help, that way you can call
for me.
They could feel his presence fly away as he went looking for the camp they had set up a

What should we do? Andy asked with proper caution.

They waited for it to rise out of the sand, and when it did, they did not like the look of it

The dream Andy had about the ash getting washed away was fresh in his mind, and he

The view suddenly zoomed far into an area. It was of a burnt city and of ruined stone. It
zoomed in on more towns and more things that had been destroyed, that would not just be

town. The creature took him through every part of it. They were too high for the water to pass
through, so they would not be touched by the water. Then Andy remembered the rain and
looked up in his view, even though he was not able to see the sun.
The shadowy thing seemed to laugh from outside the view. The scenery suddenly shifted
up towards where he had seen the sun. The sun was rising in his dream, but Andy never took
heed of where he could see the sun rising. His town was on the east side of the land, and the sun
rose in the east. That meant since they could see the sun, there were no clouds to dump the rain
as it had been in the other regions. When Andy stopped and thought, he realized that their town
would not be renewed by the water, even the land would not be touched by the cleansing water.
Now he understood, and it angered Andy, as well as creating a deep crater in his soul.
Understanding things did not always mean that you liked what that meant for you. He knew he
had to live with the meaning of the dream, but if that was what the magnificent dream meant,
then he would have to do what was right, even if the outcome was not what was best for him.
The shadow withdrew, and the dawn seemed to be approaching behind it. It stood in
front of the tent, and they had set it up towards the east. As Andy watched, the shadow faded
like the sand into the approaching sun. The glow above the horizon gave them a strength they
had not felt before. They had been able to do many things without the sun during the night, but
they had never been so grateful for the sunrise.
They were not so tired at the moment from being up all day, but they knew they should
move while they had the strength. If they did not, who knew what else lurked in the sand? When
they had packed up and sorted everything out, they got on the horses and tried to make the best
time they could.
As the day went on, weariness creeped into Andy’s limbs and he started to close his eyes,
albeit it was involuntary. Andy told them, and they said that they would like to also take a rest.
They could not stay for more than one hour, but they welcomed the rest as much as water. They
had not been able to pay attention to their water as they had ran in the night, but they had been
able to realize the things they had failed to before.

As Andy woke up, he forgot any dreams he had that night. But he knew he had had at

They did travel as much as they could, though that was not a great distance. They made a

The monster returned again that night again like a recurring nightmare. The shadow was

When they were released from the trance a second time with the great shadow, they were

hue inside of it.
The hue that glowed out of the container was more than just color, but it was also

he looked away, and the feeling disappeared. When he turned toward the others to tell them to

What was that great ball of lightning? How did it get here, and what is it doing? Then a horrible

They continued along, though they encountered a separate barrier with red inside. And

Could it be the diamond dragon’s region, since the fire was extinguished, or was it

They were able to make it past the fires and were able to make a good distance past

for the regions, but they encountered a ninth chamber, it was with a black, dark, festering globe

that might be able to help them.
They had to learn about, and understand the fires before they could go after the black
one. They knew that if they were to go about defeating the shadow of fire, they would have to go
to all the regions and find all the fires. The realization that they would have to do such gave
them some caution and pause. They were cautious because they were not prepared, but they

They made it past the last one, and there were no more that day, though they thought

When they reached the night's descent, they set up their camp and were not disturbed by

When they were able to sleep that night, they slept the best they had slept in a long time, for

Of death
It was not the poem that made him more wary, but it was the great alert that filled him as
the poem went through his mind. He heard the sound of the slipping foot again. The sound only
enhanced his thoughts and alertness. He looked around to see if he would see anything from the
things that surrounded him. If he made a mistake, or was too loud in his fast walk, he could be
caught or discovered by the thing in the dust.
The cloud of dust obscured his vision, and he was not able to see his hand now, he had to
feel in front of him in order to tell if there was an obstacle. Andy picked up his pace more,
because of his wariness, then the footsteps gained in quantity as well, and grew louder. What
had triggered them to move faster? Was it his moving faster, was something copying him, or was
it something else? It might have been the way the sound reflected off the dust, but then he
remembered, the dust had only gotten thicker.
Suddenly, he emerged from the dust and looked around a clearing made of glass. He
slowly walked toward the center of the clearing, and he could hear the others following. They
approached the center of clearing when a small tree was also emerging, it was devoid of green.
Andy stared at it for a long time and was not sure what his reaction should be. He stared into its
eyes and he was able to cause a reaction from the tree. It shuffled and it transformed into a small
green creature with yellow running along its face and body.
The creature made a gurgling whistling sound and others of the creatures came into the
clearing. They were small and looked the same, though they bore one difference, they all stood
on the opposite side of the clearing when they came in, some were even behind them, all staring
at the three in the center.
Andy was able to find a pattern in their organization as they walked, they followed the
movement of the first one. The other reaction time was slightly delayed by the first one, but the
first one followed him. Andy was amused somewhat by this, and he drew his sword, just to see
what they would do. Liam also drew it, for he wanted to be ready in case a fight broke out. There
were about fifteen of the creatures, and the first one maintained his stare with Andy. What
would they do to get out of this one?
When Andy drew the sword, the creatures less than half his height also drew small
swords, in proportion to them, but they still looked deadly. The small sharp blades were shiny,
but they were not the same as Andy’s. If they were going to attack, they needed to be fast, but
then Andy wondered if the creatures might feel the touch of his mind. When he tried to reach
the creature, he encountered a personality that he could recognize, though he could not feel its
mind. He tried to go around it, but the block followed. Andy then realized what was happening,
the creature was reaching out too but was not letting it get to its mind.
Andy knew that they would have to move closer to the center of the clearing so he could
talk to Nia with his mind. Andy was closer to the center, and the others circled close around him
when the creatures came closer to the center too.
Andy reached backwards, towards Nia’s mind. While I reach out to the one closest to me, the
first one we saw, I need you to reach around his defenses and ask him a question.
Alright, I will try.
When Andy held the creature back, he could feel Nia’s mind reaching around, and it was
working, so she was able to ask it the question. Though Andy was trying to hold the creature
back, he knew what Nia would ask. Andy was able to sense a reaction in the part of the creature
that was reaching out, but it did not recoil. It started to withdraw, but then totally withdrew, all
without breaking eye contact with Andy.
When it withdrew, the creature was able to talk openly with all of them. Andy did not
know what else Nia might have said, but it seemed to relax the creature, and it talked with them
comfortably.
What is your business here? It asked them all at once.
We must find the dragon who lives in this region. He is key to our quest, and we must find him
quickly. Was Andy’s response.
What is this quest you claim to have involving a dragon, the only dragon here is in bones about
fifty miles away from here. They have long since vanished from the land.
Well, then, we have another question to ask of you; What were the great chambers that were filled
with fire. There were nine of them.
They are the symbol of the land, they have been there for a long time, over eight hundred years,
we are its protectors, as well as the shadow creatures you most likely encountered. One word of caution
though, you must beware of the Wincher Wales, they are the black balls of light, and they sing. By the
time anyone hears their singing though, it will be too late. Some of those though, you must be extra
cautious of. They are white, and they have the will of ten of the black, they can often blend in in the
daytime, so you must be careful. They are rare, but don’t grow too confident.
We know about the Wincher Wales, but we were able to beat them with our minds.
The surprise in the mind of the creature was obvious, and the wonder also was
prominent. The creature seemed to finally look away from Andy to glance at the other of his
race. When he looked back, he asked him to follow him where he led, and started right off. The
response was so quick that he was almost not able to hear the creature.
They followed the creature, and wherever it walked, the dust fell on the floor and
gathered there. The dust that surrounded them then filled in the gap as they walked past. If they
stumbled, the dust waited until they were through before it was in the air again. When Andy
tripped, he was not happy that he had, but he did know that the dust waited, so he had the
others go in front of him, so he could keep an eye on them.
After he was all the way through the dust, following the creature, along with the others,
they realized that they had been in such a hurry to leave, that the horses had been left. Andy
mentioned it to the green gold creature who only gave a nod
The creature later said, “Our people will take care of them and find them for you.”
Andy was grateful for the will of the creatures, and he knew that they were more than
capable of finding the horses. They continued walking for a distance until they stopped and
Andy looked to see where the creature had gone, or done. He had stopped by a large column of
stone that they could not see above, for the dust obscured the view to it. When the creature
walked around the pillar several times to clear the dust, Andy knew they had arrived at the area
where they needed to go, where the center of the city was.
When the area was clear, the creature whistled, and more of the creatures grew
themselves out of the ground out of dust, and came to help him. Together, they lifted the
massive pillar and underneath was a great dark hole. The hole had an unknown depth, but he
knew that it would not be smart to jump down this one as they had before. The creatures
jumped down, including the first one that was leading them, but one remained to help them
down the hole.
The hole was about three feet wide, and it matched exactly to the pattern of the column.
The creature turned to them, and it asked them if they would like to be lowered down, or if they
wanted to jump on their own.
“We would like to be lowered, though how will you do so?” Nia asked with a curious
look. Everyone one of them wanted to know as well.
Alright, but give me a moment. The tree went into the ground as if its growth was reversed,
and then it called to them again with its mind, Over in the hole, I will lower you.
They marched over to the hole and they could see that there was a platform grown out of
it that was big enough for them to stand on. When they walked on, as they assumed they were
supposed to, the creature lost its grip and fell a ways before catching itself and digging its
rootlike legs into the wall.
You are heavier than my kind, I was not expecting the weight. It carried them down the rest of
the way with an obnoxious noise, as if his roots were moving through the rock and then placing
them back with great speed. It was not a long time that they had to wait, but when they got to
the bottom of the hole they could see light. The unexpected was that the tree suddenly unrooted
itself and fell. They fell with it. It was not a long drop, and they fell straight, but they landed in
water that was the temperature of the air.
Though it was not refreshing, they were certainly more alert now that they had been in
the water. The pool they landed in was a small one, but the hole seemed to keep going below
them. There was, however, a large room around them stretching for about fifty yards.
When they swam out of the pool of water, they were soaking wet, and Nia’s red hair hung
back in ropes. Andy’s hair was plastered to his head. One of the tree figures spotted them and
walked up to them. They looked around, and saw that there were countless of the green and gold
creatures. When the creature reached them, it told them where they could go to get clean or find
new clothes. New clothes would be welcome for them since they had been traveling for a while.
They also did not like the wet state they were in, so the creature told them that if they
looked near the clothing room, they would find everything that they could possibly need. They
also would need to find the dragon, so the creature pointed out its location as well. When they
went to the clothing room, Andy found that the clothes were all made in a similar fashion, with
dark brown leather and a small amount of green. When they had gotten dressed, and were ready
to meet the dragon, they made their way to the area they had first swam out of the underground
lake.
They waited for a small while there to talk to each other before going into the area which
they had to. They also had to inform Liam on what to expect, where he had never seen one of the
dragons before. When they were finished with their conversation, they went around the lake to
the opposite side that they had come out.
The dragon was in a structure that supported the ceiling. There were many other
supports, but that was the biggest one, serving as the chamber for the dragon. It seemed small
compared to the areas the other dragons had had, but it would suffice.
When they reached the chamber that was about twenty feet away from the ten sixty foot
long, and about three hundred feet across, lake, they entered without hesitation.
The room inside looked bigger than it was on the outside, yet still not large, but there
was not a dragon. There were, however, stairs. The stairs led straight into the ground, and they
were made out of clear glass. They were able to go down the stairs easily, and there was nothing
to hinder them.
When they got to the bottom, there was a room in a similar manner to the emerald
dragon’s room, the walls were made of topazes this time though. The room was about fifteen
feet long, but it stretched back farther than the other one did. The room seemed to be very cold
and the walls glowed a little, but that was probably from some sort of light. The dragon was not
in the room, though they could see many other doors that led to other areas. They did not know
if they should take those paths or if they should wait for the dragon to return.
They did not know how long it would take for the dragon to return, so they consulted,
and then decided to check the rooms, partly because they were curious about what was in them.
When Andy went into one of the ten hallways, he found a larger room that had weapons along
the wall, though the wall was not made of topazes. He looked around, and at the end of the room
in the center, there was a sword jagged, jutting out of the wall. Topaz was at that part of the
wall, and there appeared to be cracks in it where the sword pierced.
He walked to the sword where he was able to examine it closer. The sword itself had a
sapphire and topaz that seemed to be mixed, and they blended together where they touched
each other. The strange gem was in the hilt of the blade, and it shimmered with the light of the
lantern that poured from many lanterns on the ceiling.
A voice entered his mind, and he was able to sense the powerful consciousness of the
dragon. Well, I think you seem to be drawn to the most important things. Andy thought that everyone
would be drawn to cool things, but not just anyone would be drawn to the most important
things. In this Andy felt a hint of pride.
Are you aware of my friends? We must meet with you.
Yes, I am aware of them, why do you need to meet with me?
We need to talk with you so we can fight the darkness and win, the Black Fire.
Alright, I will contact your friends and we can meet in the main room with the shiny topaz.
Without a word, and went back into the room he had first entered, and he went to the

If you are the ones who have come to save the land, then you will have the answer to my question.
What is the darkness grueling in this land that tries to imitate our own?

Yes, that puts me at ease that we have three total dragons that are free. What is your plan to

All that may be of use to you is that we are one of the most powerful armies that there is. We may

have seen, we are masters at imitation.
Wait, you said that the monster imitates your own?
Yes, but lately, he has been the leader of a mighty force that wanders the desert, they take the

What do they look like?
They are black balls of light, if that is even what you call them. Have you met them, and if so,

We met them, and frankly, I do not know how we beat them, all I know is that we broke them and
they fled, we were not able to kill them, even if you are able to.
You three alone did this?
We had one other with us who is not human. Nia replied.
Who is this one?
He is a falcon by the name of Eradris.
Within the mental bond, they could feel the unbelieving thought pass through them like
a flood. They were almost concerned if the dragon would say more, but then it said, Eradris. He is
alive still? I thought he died to the Winchers. Bring him in.
They called to Eradris with their minds, and he responded very quickly, as if he had

Soon they heard the flapping of wings, the ruffling of feathers, and the landing of his

The dragon reached out eagerly, To confirm, you are Eradris, the Falnadr?
I am he. The bird replied cautiously. What is it that you need from me, or what is it that is
needed in the kingdom?
You were the wizard? You were the one who guarded the nine keys of Thardur?
Realization struck Andy like a hammer blow to the chest, and it almost knocked him

Wales when they encountered them before. Eradris would have been able to take them, but he

That is I. If you want my help, you may have it in whatever way you need. We also need to find
all of the dragons, but we need you to help us.
I would be more than happy to assist. I believe I know what your friends were thinking when they

That means we need to find where they reside and take them. Andy said with worry and a

Well, there will be more than just twenty there, and if there are more than fifty, then we will be in

I will be there to help, and you four were able to take fifteen by yourself. Together, we could
probably take twenty. The dragon said, very happy with maybe too much confidence.
They continued to discuss that which they would need if they were to confront all of the

The coin would be a needle in a haystack to find, but the sacrifice of time could mean the

They left the next day without delay so they could maximize their time. If they were

The dragon transformed into a large version of itself and they flew atop it instead of

By midday the second day, they saw a small dust storm kicked up by the wind, but it did
not get far, for rain started to fall and the sand was no longer bone dry.
16: Inoculation of Mind
The rain made the sand hard and sticky, so the sand kept together better. When they
made it to the camp site that they knew that they were close to the location, they were quick to
set up the camp. They did not want to approach it too quickly and without the light, so they
would sleep first.
They did not want to risk any mistake or mishap, it would only slow their progress. They
knew though that they would have to keep watch so they would not run into anything unwanted
in the night. Night was their vulnerable spot, but Andy did not know if the shadow creatures
would come after them that night as they slept, for they were now farther away from the city of
the tree creatures.
When they woke in the morning they were eager to find the coin, so they went as soon as
the sun peeked over the horizon. There were no mountains to block their light or slow the time
the sun pierced the sky, but they could no longer waste any time.
They, all together, went down a hill and into a pit where the coin was supposed to be, but
they could not find any structure that the coin could be in, of course it could be below them in
the sand. The sand was deep and they did not have the proper gear to search through it all.
When they could not find an answer, they all sat a while in thought, not even the dragon had
answers that would help them.
While Andy thought, he examined the sand for any small hills, or whatever would signify
a hidden structure, in the sand. He looked around them, and saw a pillar of stone that he had not
seen before. It seemed to get smaller as he looked, then he blinked and the next time he looked
the pillar was gone. Something unusual was happening, and Andy did not like it. He told the
dragon and also informed the others of what he had seen.
Nothing else strange seemed to happen after that, then Andy glanced back at where the
pillar had been and it seemed to rise out of the sand.
Calling for the others, he said, “Over there!” It was all he could think to say, but he knew
that it might soon disappear. But instead of disappearing it got larger. Andy moved closer to the
pillar, and he saw something that was surely the answer to his question. The pillar was not
rising and falling, the sand was. As the realization struck him, so did a gust of wind.
When Andy looked in the direction the wind was coming from, he noticed the land, and
all of it started waving, the dunes were no longer, but they moved and fell in a faster time than
they had before. Andy had seen the dunes move over night, but never this quickly. Then the
commotion spread, it waved over to them, and the sand started to give way beneath Andy’s feet.
He ran back the way he had come to look at the pillar, and the others were already experiencing
the strange phenomena’s effects.
He ran to Nia to protect her and they could stabilize each other, but they soon gave up
and sat on the ground. They thought they could still see the area that the coin was supposed to
be and they could also maintain their position. The tree creatures locked their roots, and dug
them into the sand as deep as they could in order to stay safe from the sand.
When the sand stopped, as soon as it had begun, they were confused as to the cause of
them. They had come so quickly, and they had gone away, but it was not entirely bad, for they
could then see a disturbance in the sand. The landscape was the same as it had been before the
waves, but they also helped in a strange way.
Below them in the pit, the sand was moving, sinking. It just kept getting sucked down,
and it never stopped. Its area increased and it started to suck the sand dune they were standing
on into the pit. The coin may be uncovered now, but they would just have to wait until the sand
cleared. As they looked for a sign of the coin though, or anything the coin could be in, they only
found that the sand continued to sink. They did begin to see other structures like the one they
had seen before start to be uncovered. The pit consumed a seemingly endless amount of sand,
and the structures continued to get taller.
The structures grew in height, as well as growing in length and width. The structures
were of all kinds, there were ordinary homes, there were also tall buildings that toppled because
they had lost their support when the sand drained. Finally, a small structure emerged from the
area the coin was supposed to be. It was a dome, slightly pointed at the top and it was low to the
ground, as if it was a shelter of some kind.
They looked at the creatures, and the creatures looked back at them. They slowly
approached the dome that they knew they would not be able to ignore. If they lost this chance,
they would likely not receive another one like it. They searched around the dome and it was
about twenty feet long and the same distance wide. As they searched, they saw strange hardened

or if they wanted to know.
Andy heard a call from the other side of the dome and rushed over to see what Liam had

“What is it that you have found? I can not see anything.”

The sand that was below them could easily be dug out, but Andy did not know that they

They all began digging with their hands, for they had not a shovel to work with, nor any

When they moved for the dragon, and it started digging, buckets worth of sand flew out

The hole that was there was held up by the friction that the sand had because of its wet

Andy slid into the hole on the wet sand, and examined the door. He tried pushing on it,

The others, Nia and Liam, were pushing from the top of the hole at the part of the door that was

They heard the creaking in the door, but the stone made slow progress, though it was

He exited the room so he could see to get a torch from his bag, but he was not able to

Once the torch was lit, they were able to see much more than before, and they were also

As they walked toward the ring, they noticed many other rings around the room, and

“Choose carefully, for we may not have another chance, we may not even have the chance
to think about the outcome either.”
They all examined the rings, and they all had different patterns on them. The rings were
of many different kinds and sizes, but some were totally plain. There was one that was golden,
another that was silvery, and more materials besides, though they knew that there would only be
one. Something protected as much as this room would surely have more protection inside.
Andy saw more than a hundred rings, but they all looked strange, or too intricate. He

detail as clearly on it. Though he could see the detail clearly, it felt as if he did not see it at all.
He remembered briefly that the ring they were looking for would take away the strength of the
minds of the opponents to whoever wielded it. Andy reached for the ring, and whenever he got
closer, it felt more nonsensical to let himself do something like that. He was able to reach it, and
as soon as he touched it, the feeling went away, but he could still feel that the others were drawn
to it and pushed from it as he had been.
Andy wondered how he could remove the attraction to the ring, but they would just stare
at it with a confused expression. They were almost afraid of the ring in his hand, but he could
not think of a way to stop the effect on all of them. He thought of something, and he reached
with his mind toward the ring to see what it would do or if it would do anything.
He felt a small resistance, but he was able to breach it, and when he did, he was able to
give it instructions as to who to direct its attacks. He told it not to attack whoever he smiled at,
and then he withdrew his mind, and smiled at each one of them in turn. He immediately saw the
difference in their countenance. They looked as if they had broken from another trance, and
perhaps it was a trance, but Andy knew what it was like if so. They all smiled back at him, and
Andy started to laugh at them, because of how they all did it at once. They were able to break
the trance, get outside, and show it to the world. When they were outside though, the most
important thing was that they had chosen the right ring, and knew that they had chosen one
with the right ability.
Andy was happy when he got out of the pit though, and he used a system to get out of the

There was a low chance that anyone would be able to find the hole, even if they wanted

They could only prevent risk by getting the job they now had in front of them done.
They traveled back to the city very speedily, but this time a storm blew with them, and

When they arrived at the city, they were able to resupply themselves and Andy had to also find a

He dove into the hole, and then he waited to be submerged by the welcoming water. The

Andy emerged into a different room that he could see more in, and it was the same size
