that not one was around, for he wanted to explore the rest of the dragon's room, or at least that
is what he assumed it was.
He went into the only exit there was to the room, and sure enough, there was the topaz
walled room that glowed. The other doorways were unexplored by him, so Andy decided to go
left to right. When he went into the first room from the left, there were many chests lying about
the room and also many lanterns that lit the room well. The room was very clean as if the dragon
made sure every bit of grime was totally cleaned, perhaps it meant more to him than other
rooms. Andy went to look into one of the chests, but he was not able to do so, for there must
have been a spell placed upon it.
When Andy moved to the next room, there were many crests around the room, and he
assumed they were the crests of kings in the region, or those who were respected. Andy
wandered in the room for a while examining the intricate designs of each of the crests, and
maybe he would recognize one.
In the third room, Andy found many small tables shorter than his waste that held small
trinkets of certain use. Some of the trinkets were a coin, a small dagger, an earring, and another
was a small pin. They all had a feeling of magic to them, but they were not as powerful as other
things Andy had seen. There were more things that were not of as much interest in Andy's
mind, but they were still magical.
In the next room, there were only chairs in a circle and it appeared to be a counseling or
discussing room. There was not much to see in the room, but there were many tapestries and
banners on the walls that could have been for decoration, or maybe something else.
In the fourth room, there was quite an empty area, as well as in the fifth and sixth. In the
seventh room, there was a pedestal with a gem on it. The gem was yellow like the main rooms
topaz, but the gem did not appear to be topaz. The gem had an intricate pattern inside of it, but
it was not like other gems that he had seen.
The gem glowed with a pale light, a grayish light, and the light seemed to be important.
The light reminded him of the rock region, and seemed to have some sort of connection to the
region. Andy wondered what the light would do, or the gem would do if he touched it. He was
close enough to do so, so he reached toward the gem. There was no resistance, and he could not
feel magic around it, rather it felt as if its magic was far away.
It was as if the gem was wary of him, and like it had a consciousness. He felt the
resistance of the consciousness, but he could not talk to it. The thought of taking the gem was
not a prospect that pleased Andy, however, he did know that he could instruct it as he had with
the ring. What would the gem do though? Was it closed off to anyone who did not own it?
He walked around the gem trying to take in all the detail, but it did not provide any new
insights. The gem would give nothing more than the outward appearance. If he could wield the
gem though, if he could pick it up, or if he dared, he may regret it.
In the next room, there was a room without anything, however it was strange, and it gave
him a feeling of being watched, or as if someone was aware of what he did in the room. The
feeling reminded him of other feelings he had had, but they were not as clear as this one was. He
decided to leave the room, for it gave him an awkward feel.
The next room was the one he had been in before, but he knew that there was something
different about the room. It seemed as if something had been removed, or something had been
added, though Andy could not tell which. He looked around at the blades and weapons again,
but he could not find the thing that was missing. He came across one of the displays that seemed
like it had had something in it before, but others too had the same feel about them. Andy could
not find the cause of the uneven or missing feeling, but again, as he was looking at a weapon, he
heard the voice of the dragon.
He turned to see the dragon standing there with a sword in its mouth. The sword was
short, but it shined, it was the one with the yellow and blue gem in it. He knew he had seen
something more the first time he was in the room. He admired the gem in the sword especially
as the dragon's presence pressed in on him and said, You may have been looking for this. You
admire it.
Yes, it is beautiful.
I can tell you about it if you wish. It is a long story, but it is one of my most prized possessions.
Yes, please tell me.
Alright, I acquired this weapon about two hundred years ago. To be blunt, I got it from a great
enemy. It was a shadow enemy, one that digs through you to find your fears. The shadow was in human
form, and he was a mighty, formidable opponent. It was dawn that I encountered him while I walked in a
calm puddle. There were puddles still then, but it is no more to be. The fire shadow wanted to make the
land vile in so many ways, so he created all of the regions. When I looked into the water, there appeared
to be clouds blowing over me, but when I looked at the sky, nothing was present.
I sensed something was wrong, so I surveyed my surroundings to see if there was anything that
could do me harm. That is when I saw my opponent. At this point, I was human in form. The Saps taught
me how to shapeshift, and I was in my human form. The shadow that I had seen in the water was only to
get my attention, but then I realized its dual purpose.
As I watched the shadow, the person I had seen disappeared. I looked around, though I could not
see where it was until, out of thin air shadow appeared and it solidified into the one I had seen.
The shadow walked toward me with a sneer upon his face and his eyes curved into a glare. When
he got to me, he stopped, and I noticed the shiny sword with the beautiful hilt. I wanted the sword, but I
knew it would not be wise to go against one that I did not know the power of. When we engaged in
combat, bashing our swords against the others, it was not the best business, though it was necessary.
We fought for a while, but he eventually gained the upper hand and pushed me into the water to
take me. At that moment, I saw him turn his sword in a peculiar way, as if he were going to take my life.
That direction in which he turned the blade though, allowed me to jab it through his ribs.
Is that all? Andy asked with a bit of impatience.
Yes, that is all I have to say. Remember it too.
I will not forget. Andy replied, but probably would.
What is the sword, and what does it do? Is it special or magical?
Yes, it has the power to control the flow of water, no matter where it may be. The dragon
paused, looked at the blade between his teeth, then walked it over to the pedestal it had been on.
He then went to the others and blew upon all of them. When he was done, he went out the door,
but before he crossed the threshold, he looked back at Andy, then continued. Andy was not sure
of the meaning of the look.
He followed the dragon out of the doorway and met Nia there too, but Liam was absent
from the room. He wondered at the location of Liam, but he did not ask yet. The dragon went to
the place that he had originally been when they had first met him, but he appeared to be a little
more humble than the times before.
Just then, Eradris flew into the room and landed on the ground beside Andy and Nia.
When Andy found that Eradris was talking to the dragon at the same time as talking to them,
and in an urgent way, he also grew anxious. Andy did not know if he was able to assume
correctly about the matter, but the way that they talked seemed urgent.
I have seen many of the Wincher Wales out and about, but I cannot guess its purpose, or any plot
that would be behind it. I know that they were all gathered closely, and that they were wailing, but I do
not know anything about anything else.
The dragon replied with urgency as well, I know that they may want to attack, for they have
done so in the past, though they may overpower us if there are so many as you say. I think we may have to
strike while they strike us. If we act quickly and cleverly, we will be able to pull it off. Now, we need to
plan. If we do not have a good plan, we will not be able to succeed. Now, what will we need and how will
we approach?
They planned for the next few hours and continued into the night with their planning.
The three were able to see many of the dragon's counselors and, of course, the war chief or chief
of war, however it was pronounced, was the most helpful. When they were done, it was well past
the middle of the night, but they still needed to plan more. If they failed, who would be able to
help them then? They could never do enough planning, though they reached a point that they
needed to stop.
They went to bed that night in a strange bed, but it felt wonderful, for they had not slept
properly in a good while. If they were going to be the most successful, they would also need the
rest for the attack. That night though, Andy had a dream, and it seemed so real, real enough.
He dreamed that the Wincher Wales did not attack and they were able to attack first, but before
they did, the effects started happening. They saw the desert become lush again, and the sand become a
water oasis. There was still sand in some areas, but those areas were not hot and miserable as this desert
was. The shadow and confused people still wandered in the desert, the shadow creatures still chased you,
but they did it all as a joke.
At the end of the dream, Andy’s perspective was reversed, and he turned on the shadow people,
the wanderers ran from him. He felt himself growing taller, though the ground did not appear far down,
nor did the air feel colder, but the sensation of death came upon Andy, even though he had not known
before what it was like. The thought of destroying the land came over him, and he suddenly,
uncontrollably started doing so. He was content in the dream, and he was not able to change his
emotions.
He came upon his friends, and they only stared up at them as if they stared at a mountain. He
reached down to pick them up, but he stopped and then the dream stopped too.
Andy jolted awake and he looked at the ceiling of the small room he was in, though he
was not at all feeling rested. He wondered how long he had slept, but he knew it could not have
been long. He laid back down in his bed, knowing that he would need the sleep, but he would
not be able to get it. He wondered at the meaning of the dream, but he knew that he would never
do the things in the dreams that were implied in the dream. He would not destroy the land, he
would not do any of it, and he would never hurt his friends.
Sometimes, Andy wondered why he had certain dreams, but he knew that they were only
his worries about the world. The brain was a powerful thing that could conjure insane things
and bring into the world the worst ideas. Andy sat and thought about all that he saw, and he was
not able to get back to bed until late into the morning.
When he woke up again, he was eager again to get going and on the road. When he went
to the area outside the palace that they would be meeting, he found that the others were already
there, except for Nia, who was with him.
They all went together after awhile of refreshing everyone on the plan that they had,
but it seemed that they were all eager to be going. They were able to get a good distance that
day, but the course of the Wincher Wales had changed. With the help of Eradris, they were able
to find that the Winchers were then going southwest. They were going directly west before, and
they wondered why the course changed.
They readjusted their course to find the Winchers, but they were on horses, because they
had also discussed where Andy, Nia, and Liam were going to go after they were done with the
Winchers, and they decided that they would want to go into the mountains and find the lava
region, the smallest region of all the regions so they could maximize their time, while also
making their way south back to the rock region.
They were able to ride hard on their steeds and able to reach a fourth of their
destination, when they realized that the shadow creatures did not come out that night to assail
them, neither did the giant shadow. Andy was confused at the occurrence, but he knew they had
to keep going with confidence, or they would lose their will against the Wincher Wales. All
depended on them and the moment that awaited them.
They were not able to go as far the next day, but the important thing was that they
moved. When they set up camp the second night, they did not see the creatures that night either,
but they did notice that there were darkening clouds above them. The clouds were superior, and
they saw everything below them. The sky was totally covered with grey clouds by the morning of
the third day of their trip, but they kept going, even when the rain broke out in the desert.
Nothing else was out of sorts as they traveled, but they were hindered by the rain. They
were also glad for the cooling that the rain provided them in the daytime, blocking the sun's
rays from reaching them. They were able to make a good distance even in the rain, though they
were not as aware because of the sound it gave off. They were walking in the rain for a while,
long enough to be soaked to their bones, but they knew that the cold and damp could be worse
than the rays of the sun as it continued longer.
Luckily, the weather seemed to balance out in the middle of the fourth day, and they
were able to see better, as well as be dryer for once. They had a good time going in the rain, but
the sun could also be welcoming. When they made it to the end of the fourth day, they could see
the mountains, but they could also see the Winchers on the horizon. The Winchers were stealthy
and they made little noise, but occasionally, they would let out a shrill cry.
They were able to plan their attack based on the movement and the rate at which the
wincher wales would have to go to escape them into the mountains. If the Winchers got into the
mountains, it would be dangerous business for them if they were separated. Most importantly,
they had to stay together. If they were separated, it could mean the end of their entire quest.
They attacked early on the fifth morning, and they approached early, so the sun was
barely throwing its light over the horizon. They got to the Winchers, but they were not able to
get very close without being detected.
The battle would now begin.
17: Battle Between Us
How could they beat the might of the Winchers? They had strong minds and large
numbers, but there was also something different about them this time. Instead of the practical,
planned for, black light, there were Wincher Wales with a white light, and it made them look
more ominous and intimidating. They seemed to possess more power.
They approached, and the Wincher Wales held their ground with their red and purple
eyes. They were composed, as if they had more confidence. The coin was also now with them, so
they also bore more confidence. The confidence that they had would have to hold, for they also
needed to experience the power of the white ones. They engaged minds.
The power of the Wincher Wales hit them in full force and they had to physically brace
themselves for the onslaught of attacks that happened so suddenly. It felt like hundreds of
pounding fists pounding at their minds, but somehow, they felt themselves holding their own.
They were able to keep themselves in the moment, and the others were doing well, however,
Andy started to lose confidence at the malicious confidence of the Winchers. They were doing
so well, yet it felt like they were playing with them.
Andy sank into a deep state of thought and contemplation, though he was not low. He
held his sanity, but he felt something seeping into his heart, as well as his mind. It was a cold
feeling, though he knew that he could fight it. Even though he could fight it, he chose not to, he
wanted to feel it, though the feeling was not his own. Down he went, the feeling like rain in a
freezing temperature, and solidifying around his heart. The effect started to happen to his mind
too, though he was ignoring the feeling on purpose. He could not give a reason for his
ignorance, but he knew that it would not result well in the end.
His mind slowed, his heart seemed to skip beats and he thought vile thoughts turning
against himself. He felt as if he would rather end the pain and suffer death than endure the pain
he faced at this moment, but then the humility returned to his mind, and he remembered that he
could have such a great trait. He could have the trait, and he had seen it in himself before. Look
at what he had done. He was able to defeat two of the fires, though he needed help, he could feel
the growth inside. He saw the determination that was inside, and he longed for it, but still he
sunk. Was this his fate, was this his cut, his end? If it was, he would fight it. The impossible
things could be done, he had seen it. In the things he had heard, seen, felt. Many of the things he
had felt during his journey, he thought they were not possible, though they succeeded. He now
knew that they could win. He could get to the end, achieve his quest.
I see myself
My desire
I am strong,
I am great.
Time may wait no longer! We will not give without a fight, we always remember, never surrender. We are
the ones, the ones in the water, the ones, we reflect. We talk with the untalkable, think about the
undoable, and grow through a stubborn world. Who are we if not those who fight for right and truth?
What is it that we know, believe, and hold to?
Show me your light
I give my sight
We will work timelessly!
Remember your future is bright!
The time is right!
Unleash your will
Your strength

But never give your heart to them who seek to destroy the power of it!
We are not weak
We flow like the creek
We fly in the sky
Not sit with a sigh
Raise your head!
Remember your promises!
And fight for your faith unyielding!
Andy jolted back into his mind. He felt the Winchers presence close to his own, and the
others were fading in their will, even Eradris. They needed help. He remembered all that he had
heard. The message of hope and faith. Remember your promises! He had made himself a
promise, which could be the most important ones he had made on the journey. He had promised
to vanquish the enemy, to fight to the last man.
He suddenly threw a mental punch like the thunder he had heard when his house and
town had burned. He threw the confusion, hate, disdain, sadness, or otherwise, passion into the
punch. Instead of a punch though, they thought the power of it was so strong that the Winchers
were disoriented and they did not have the foggiest idea of how they had been thrown back, they
were so close, yet so was Andy all the times he failed. What could he have done to reverse the
failure?
Instead of accepting the punch from the world, he took its arm and threw the hate he
had experienced from it and threw it on the ground. He reversed it on the enemy. Power was
power, it could be used for good or bad, but only those with the will and faith in their friends
and others could accomplish the will. They could throw many punches or gain much ground, but
that ground that all of his failures gained, was also the ground he gained, for he now knew how
to counter all of the winds in the storm.
When the Winchers were organized again, Andy did not even wait for them to engage, he
threw the passion again at them. They were more prepared that time, but he knew that he could
take them. Knowledge was power, but what knowledge you gain is the power that you receive.
He did not wait, he showed no mercy, and he could feel the help of the others at his side. They
were fighting with a renewed vigor as he did, but they were driving the force. Without them, he
would not be able to go about the attack. The Wincher Wales minds were low, and they seemed
to be weak.
Through a pause in the mental link that the five shared, Andy said, Now, or never!
They all attacked with a mighty punch, as if they combined their power into one massive
fist. The power that was behind the punch gave Andy more passion and determination. Even
above them as they thought of the fist, an orange first appeared, a ghostlike fist, but when it
struck, the Winchers did not budge against the power of the fist. They seemed stunned,
paralyzed in their light.
With a strange feeling to it, the Winchers started to squeal, and that is when the five
broke contact to watch with their own eyes. They watched, and as they did, the Winchers
crumpled and squealed. They seemed to lose their dark light, and the white ones shuddered.
They did not dare touch the Winchers, but they backed away, and a good thing too.
As they backed away from the Wincher Wales, they heard the squeals increase, and then
they exploded with a pang to the ears. The noise was so loud that they could barely hear after
that. Though they knew that it would return, for the explosions was only one that was meant to
hinder them. Andy did not know how he knew, but the thought just came to him, and he
accepted it as if he were welcoming a long lost friend to a party.
Andy was filled with joy, he had found much and discovered much in the time of the few
minutes he had been unconscious to the world.
He learned so much in only that amount of time, so he knew he would need to write it
down, however, he did not have anything to write with. Andy was able to think of a way to
remember, if he told others of the things he had learned, they would likely remember, especially
if they were very wise. He felt that he could trust Eradris, and he trusted his wisdom the most.
He decided to tell him, and they exchanged the memory with a few short explanations.
You have grown much in only a short time, I am proud that you are learning. I will keep this
knowledge safe, and I will never forget it.
Thank you, Andy was truly thankful, and he even made a gesture through his mind to give
the bird a small hug. He did not know why he felt to do so, but his joyful feeling fueled it. When
they released the contact, Andy saw the others who were staring at him with their eyebrows
slightly down, but wide smiles across their faces.
He reflected their smiles, but he ran to hug them, and he did not know why he did that
either, but he was happy that he did so, for he felt that he could feel their passion pouring out of
them. He was more grateful for their presence than he had been before, and he knew they felt
the same way.
When they were released, they saw the dragon waiting for them, as well as Eradris. They
were able to talk freely for a while, but then they moved to more serious matters. The matters
were about the journey into the mountains. They would need transportation, and they would
also need to communicate with each other. The horses were there, though they had stopped
them a far way off from the Winchers. As for the communication, the dragon produced another
mirror like the one that they had previously seen.
You are stronger than I expected, and you contain multitudes within you. Many emotions, many
complex ones too. Be careful though, for you can be controlled by your emotions. Remember, true
direction comes from your heart, your feelings are just the means of communication to others, as well as
yourself, The dragon said to Andy, very seriously. He knew that the dragon meant it, but he also
knew that the dragon contained a little jealousy.
Thank you. It means much to me, and I still have much to learn, though I did feel something
inside I have not felt before. I feel that I want to grow, I want to learn, and most importantly, that I have
the will to do so. My strongest desire is to keep my promise to the land and do that which is the best for it.
I love the land I was born in, and I will protect it to my dying day, whether I die in the process of
defending it or until time takes me.
The dragon then made sure that Andy’s mirror worked for them and they could talk
through it. The most important thing was that they get rest and that they were able to process
what they had seen and done through the day. They got the well deserved rest, and they went to
sleep with hope and faith that the future would turn out. Andy knew it would because he knew
to fight for the faith he cherished.
The battle was over, but not the war, they still had to get going on their way into the
mountains. In the mountains, they would have to go to more cautious extents because they
would be in a more rainy area. The mountains they had encountered before were very tall, but
these would be a third the size, and a little more easy to traverse.
They traveled the rest of the way to the mountains, and they soon parted ways with the
dragon, so they knew they would have to do anything that they should do before they would not
be able to. The dragon did give them one last thing though, and Andy was not aware that he had
had it. The dragon gave them the gem that Andy had seen in the room before, though he did not
know if the dragon had anything else.
It will help you not to lose your way. If you ever lost your map, but you put information into this
you will be able to keep it as long as you wield the gem. If you keep the gem close to you, you will be able
to store almost an infinite amount of information. It will also not be easily separated from you the more
information you store. It is a useful tool.
“Thank you,” Nia said out loud as well as with her mind.
“Yes, we appreciate this.” Andy also thanked the dragon.
If it will help you, I am happy to give it.
After that, they went into the mountains so they could make the best time with the light
that they still had. If they were going to accomplish their goal to save the land, they would need
to conserve all the time they could.
In the mountains, it would be harder for them to find the dragon because they did not
know where to start looking. Andy thought it would be best to go directly into the mountains,
and none of the others had a plan besides, so they left right away.
They were off in only a little while, and they were glad to have their horses so that they
did not have to tire their legs or feet as much. They did not find such a good place to rest as they
had had in the past, but they did find a tree to camp under. They tied the horses to a tree nearby
and they made their small fire under a large pine so that they could have a somewhat defensible
position.
They had prepared for their venture into the mountains with more food, as well as
getting small knives for hunting as well as cleaning the animals. Andy would not do the cleaning
part unless he had to, but luckily for him, Liam had done it before. Andy was able to keep his
cheerful mood too, so they were greater in their satisfaction.
They were able to sleep well that night, and Andy had no dreams. He seemed more
content and calm in his thoughts than he had before, though he knew that, as he had thought
and said, he still had much to learn. He knew that he would need to learn more skills, though the
skill he had with his mind could be the most useful one. But Andy had felt something inside
himself, something very vast and he was eager to learn it. Magic.
He had no idea how to start, all he knew was that he could learn it and was capable of it,
he wondered though, who could teach him.
He talked about the night they fought the Winchers about the things he had felt, but not
all of them. He dare not share all of his feelings and reveal so much of himself to others. He was
not willing to go so far as to do that, though he did share the feeling he had with the magic.
Liam took immediate interest in the thought, and he told them that he could do a little
magic and he knew some that would be easy enough. Andy thought the prospect could be
engaging, even if it was Liam who taught it, though he wondered what kind of magic he could
do.
While they were on the road, they talked about how magic worked, “Magic comes from
thought. If you can think of something, you can perform it, but you need to have the proper
energy. Without energy, you would not bring anything to pass, therefore, your energy is used to
fuel the spell you cast. You can get energy from other things,but you would have to direct the
energy, whereas you do not have to do so if the energy comes from your own body.”
“What if you are out of energy and you cast a spell?” Andy asked.
“It depends on the spell, it could kill you if you cast one big enough, though others will
leave you only unconscious, though that is not any better if you are in an emergency.”
“How do we use it? Are there special things you have to say?”
“The things to say, yes, but not as complicated as you might think. It only takes a word in
the category of your knowledge. What that means, is that you only have to know the word for the
category of spell it is in. That narrows it down, but there are still many words you can use aside
from those categories.”
“What is a word that I could practice with?”
“The easiest word that I know would be the word Nvastok. It means ‘bring heat’. You can
use it for starting fires.”
“Alright, I will try.”
“Wait, you need to direct it at something. If you do not direct it at something, it will
become a wild magic, and that type of magic may not even do the action you tell it to. Remember
to think of the word as well.”
Andy said no more and he focused on the task. Andy looked at a nearby sapling that
would have died anyway because of the area it was growing. He said the word and directed the
thought as Liam had told him. The tree started to smoke slightly and acquired a small amount of
blackened bark. The bark only stayed like that, and he tried again, focusing on the heat and
stressing the thought as if he were trying to talk to an animal. That time, the tree blackened
further and then acquired a small flame halfway up the trunk. It did not spread up the trunk very
quickly and went out because of the wet wood.
“Good, very good. It is nice when you have someone to help you. You can always learn
better when you have encouragement. I had to learn by myself and figure out how to perform
the magic and that is where I learned to talk with my mind.”
It was then that Andy felt the drain in his own energy, and it was more than he expected.
He felt the drain from all over his body, even in his arms and legs, “That drain was more than I
expected.”
Nia watched the two of them and watched as Andy practiced more with the magic. It
was an enjoyable thing. Andy wanted to learn more about the magic, but he knew that he should
conserve the energy for when he needed it later. He would also not want to use too much energy
on more magic.
The group made it far into the mountains that day, and this time, they were able to find a
cave to stay in that night. Since the spring came early that year, they would not have to worry too
much about seeing snow like they had in the tundra mountains, though it was still a possibility.
They set up the camp a little after the sky dimmed, because of the mountain's shade that
blocked the sun's light. They were not as tired in their minds that time as they were in their legs.
They had not ever ridden horses for so long, and it started to take its toll on them.
They made a small fire that night that they were able to see by, as well as fill the cave
with warmth. The cave went farther into the side of the mountain than the one they had been in
before, but it was efficient at keeping it properly warm inside. They talked around the fire and
they also talked about many things concerning the dragons and the future. They talked about
the future and what it might hold for them in the mountain region. They liked talking about the
future, though it was a somewhat frightening prospect.
When they were ready to go to sleep, they laid down in the blankets that they had
brought. They did not have a pillow though, one of the things that they had forgotten to bring.
They could lay their heads on the ground, though it would not be comfortable, and they could
sleep fine. When they were all asleep though, something happened that was quite common,
though they were not disturbed.
When they woke in the morning, they saw tracks of some great cat or animal coming
into and going out of the cave. They were worried about the cat, though grateful that it had not
bothered them in the cave. They wondered what brought the cat into the cave, whether it was its
cave, or if it was just doing its normal round. Even though they could talk to animals, they could
not talk to them if they were unaware of the cat. They were cautious that day and they watched
for a sign of danger everywhere they were. Their tense demeanor soon diminished, but they did
not lose it entirely.
Andy continued to practice the spell, and he was soon able to light the fire instantly
instead of it turning black first. Andy had a thought about something that he could do, but he
dared not try to do it, for he knew not even the slightest in what it could do.
When they were done practicing their magic for the day, Andy wanted to experiment
with the gem, and see how it worked. He reached for the gem, but it did not respond to him. He
reached many times, but nothing happened. He then tried a different approach to the problem.
He tried touching the gem and thinking all of the information that he wanted it to retain. Andy
thought that he felt something seem to open about the gem.
He reached back toward the gem with his mind, and he felt the small amount of
information he had stored. It was almost like someone else's mind, but far less powerful. It
might even be able to solve a few problems on its own.
Seeing that the method he had used worked, Andy then started to store more
information in the beautiful thing. He wanted to store all of the memories he had, but he could
only store that which he could think about. Many things he did not remember at the time, but if
someone else brought them up, he would remember. Andy did not know how to store the things
that were in his mind that he did not remember at the time.
That night, as they had to start traveling south, Andy thought about a way that he could
get the memory into the gem. He thought that it had a good chance of working, knowing that
others could see his mind if he let them.
He asked Nia if she would do it for him, and she happily agreed to do so. He was glad
that she had, because they could store both of their memories in the gem. They both knew the
importance of getting their memory and thinking stored in the gem, for if they forget the
stories, they would not have them for the most important time of their lives from the time that
they lost it.
She reached into his mind and brought more things to the remembrance as he stored it
inside the gem. The efficiency of the plan was very quick, much quicker than they had thought
they would be able to. When they were finished storing Andy’s memory, they proceeded to store
the memories of Nia in the gem. They hid nothing from each other, and no one or nothing
bothered them as they did so. They were able to get the memory stored, then they were able to
store the gem in the magic bag that held a great amount of material.
They had not run out of wood since they were in the ice kingdom. They were very
grateful for all of the things that they had been given by the dragons. They did not encounter
any more of the parties like the Celebration of Faranlas.
They were able to sleep well that night by their fire, but they also needed to get enough
sleep, so they did not stay up to talk more, but went straight to sleep. They found that they were
more tired than they realized, for they went to sleep soon after their head touched the pillow.
No dreams assailed them that night.
They woke in the morning with a renewed want to get to the south and further their
travel to the south. They moved quickly, but they encountered something that they did not
expect, even though they were likely to encounter the thing. The thing that they encountered
was a large bear with claws that appeared to be very large, larger than the size of a normal bear.
The bear was not able to be negotiated with, though it was able to hear what they tried to
say to it. What they said only enraged the bear more, though the things that they said were
meant to be nicer things. The bear attacked, and the horses spooked. They were not able to be
controlled very easily and they were not able to see where they were going. The trees were thick
enough that they did not know if they were even together, though they were likely not going in
the same direction together.
They were not going to be able to get back together, but meanwhile, Andy could not even
see, for he had both of his feet out of his stirrups and he was able only to see leaves as the
environment and trees were different.
18: The Silver Mountains
Andy finally fell off of the horse's saddle and it kept running. He had had a horse be
spooked with him on them before, but they must have been totally out of their wits, for the
horse did not stop running. Andy wondered what was so different about the bear that would
drive the horse to run like it did. The bear was big, but it was not too much bigger than normal
bears. It was a little frightening that the bear had heard them, but refused to be peaceful.
Luckily, Andy had time that he could still find a place to be safe for the night.
Andy was alone in the wilderness with no idea of what to do with the time that he had,
and he could not tell the way to go yet. He would have to pay attention to the sun so he could
find west, then he could find the north. He would need to find the north if he was going to
continue the quest, or have any hope of finding Nia and Liam. He suddenly had a wave of
loneliness upon him, though he did not know what it was, or why it came upon him. Andy was
very vulnerable without a horse, and he was not able to go in a specific direction, for he could
not see the sun well through the trees.
He decided that he would be best off if he got high enough above the trees to see the sun
and he was fairly close to the mountain side. He saw the mountain cliffs that they had followed
for the day, but he could not scale a cliff. He walked over to the cliff and he looked from side to
side to see if there was a less steep area of the cliff. To his left, there appeared to be a slight
incline change, but he was not totally sure. He knew that he would have to go there to find out
for sure, but he also knew that it could just be a trick of his eyes. He made his way over to the
area that he had seen, and it did not take him long to get there, but he was a little disappointed,
for the mountain was the same.
He went to the right, and strangely, he was surprised to find that the mountain was
decreasing in the steepness. It could be the help of the shadows that made the mountain seem
that way, but he did not know how it could have. Andy followed the mountain to the right, and it
got less steep every step. By the time he walked about three hundred feet, the mountain was flat
enough to climb up.
Andy began, and he found that as he went, the mountain got easier to climb, and it was
more flat the farther he went. Andy was grateful for the flatness of this area, though it was
hardly flat, but it gave him small, comfortable ledges to stand upon safely. Every time he
climbed, it got easier, and Andy was almost at the top after about an hour of climbing. He could
see the sun, and it was just past midday at this time, so he knew which way he would need to go,
but he still wanted to get to the top of the mountain to see the land around. It was still tiring,
but he knew the view would be worth it, as well as seeing his surroundings with the high view.
Andy was able to get the best view he had ever seen in his life, at least in reality. He
could see small rivers snaking through the valleys below him, so he was able to see everything
else too. He could see the trees and the rocks protruding. He could even see the mountains that
were lower than he was. The mountains were very small compared to the ones he had seen
before. The sun was setting behind the mountains, though he could still see them from his great
height. He felt powerful on the mountains, but he also felt helpless, for he could not find his
friends with all the power that he felt.
He looked down the mountainside, and he looked to see if there was any movement
down on the ground. Andy did not know if he would be able to find his friends again. He needed
to save the land, and quickly, but he also did not want his sister, and his friend to die. What
would he do if something befell them. He would not know what to do, neither did he want to
imagine it. The view, though, lightened his mind, and it calmed his perspective of the world.
When the sun was finally not able to be seen, Andy used the remaining light to build a
camp upon the mountain. He thought it would be a well protected area, and he would be safe
from the bigger animals. He knew that he would not be able to sleep that night, but what he
thought would be a good thing to do would be to make a bonfire to attract the attention of the
others, if they were still alive…
He got to work doing so, and he did not have the unlimited seeming wood that he could
pull out of, though he had his sword, his mind, and a little food that he had on him. He would
need to find more food, but he did not know where he would do that. He went to the few trees
that were on the top of the mountain and found that he was able to push them down, for they
did not have a strong foundation. It was more rocky on the top of the mountain than it was on
the floor where all of the sediment rested and the water pooled.
Andy was able to make a fire using two sticks, he rubbed them together until they were
warm, then he added some stringy bark, perfect for a fire starter. He was able to build it very
high, though he wondered if it would be enough. He did not even know if the others could see it,
for they had gone in the opposite direction. The fire kept him plenty warm without a blanket or
tent, for he lacked one. It was somewhere on his horse spooked into the mountain range. He
knew that he could stay warm and survive fine in the wilderness, though he did not know if he
would be able to get animals to come up the cliff to keep him company.
Andy went to his bed by a rock that had some small leaves and another smaller rock to
sleep on. He was able to rest, though not to sleep. His eyes were wide open staring into the fire
that was fifteen feet away and roaring with great height. It was about fifteen feet tall, and it was
about five feet across. Andy felt it calming to stare into the waves of the fire.
He was able to get to bed and he was not held back from sleep, rather he felt that he slept
better during that night. He was rested in the morning and ready to face the challenges that the
day could bring. He thought that he might be able to find his friends eventually, and as he was
going about that morning on the mountaintop, he heard a familiar voice, the voice that he had
heard when he was in the snow, the strange voice. It was a chilling voice that had a stinging
effect in his heart.
Remember forever, remember the fire. Remember your promise and always fight for what you
believe in. If you do, you can be more powerful than your opponent, whatever that may be. The difference
between you when you do this is that you can have all the knowledge in the world, and knowledge is
power, but if you do not believe in your cause, you gain the power to fight for it. Also, remember to gain
wisdom, for without wisdom, knowledge is near useless.
The voice resonated in his head, and he contemplated it as he stared over the valleys
below. The sun highlighted the tops of some of the mountains that were to the north and tipped
with snow. The snow on the top of the mountain glowed like silver, and they gleamed silver. The
mountains were very beautiful, but they were also blinding, for the sun was also peeking over
the mountain and reflecting its silver light out of the ice capped mountains.
He climbed down the mountain and had a direction in which he needed to go. He was
not sure if his friends would be able to find him again. He was able to get down the mountain,
and when he did, he also got the realization that he would need to go to the north if he was
going to find his friends. What about the quest? If he looked for his friends, he would have to
give up the possibility that the quest would succeed. He did not know what he could do to fulfill
both of his promises. What would he need to do, how could he choose.
If he chose either one, he would regret the consequences. What was worth it, the world,
or his friends? The thoughts inside of him were his fuel as he walked, he found berries along the
way, but he ignored them, for he was not hungry for food, he was hungry for vengeance on the
world. How would he go on, how?
He got aggravated enough to run, so ran he did, and he released his energy through the
power of his legs. His eyebrows were curved in slants as he ran, but inside, he felt as a helpless
man. He needed something that he could not remember, something like a lost dream. He knew
he had felt it before, though he could not remember where he had. When he got to a place that
seemed familiar, he knew that that had been the area that the horses spooked. He knew that
Liam would try to protect Nia, and Andy knew that, but he needed to find them.
What worried him more, was what they would choose to do. What would they do if they
expected to find him in a town, but he was not there, but then he knew that he needed to finish
the quest. When the sun was setting, and he had run far, he went in the separate direction, in the
opposite direction.
Hoe had made up his mind, he needed to follow his friends. What would they think
about him if he acted as if he did not care about them? What would he think after he was not
able to save the land? What would happen to them, plus, he would not have a chance without his
friends, they had helped him through so much. He could not abandon them, and what if the
enemy found them. They would be much safer together.
He went with more determination the next morning, though he still worried what his
trying to find them would do to the success of the quest. If he failed the quest, he failed his
friends, but if he abandoned his friends, he would regret it. He thought it would be better to die
together as they were friends rather than dying from something he could not enjoy and if he
died, he did not want to die with many regrets, especially not one that involved his family.
As he thought of his family, he wondered what might have come upon Trevar and Lairn.
If they were in danger, what would he do? What had happened to the rest of his family?
Andy ran again the next morning with even more determination than he had had the
previous morning. He ran through the day, though he still had to take rests. He knew that he
would be able to make major progress that day, but he did not know if he was actually making
progress. He knew he would travel far, but would he find his friends?
He would still try to keep his eyes open for the enemy, but he would need to get to the
friends if they were to defeat it. They needed to work together, or they might not succeed. He
knew that he had gained a very important skill in his last encounter with the Wincher Wales,
though he did not think that he could do it. Another thing he had learned was humility.
Humility was the more important one in his opinion, for he was now more aware of his flaws.
He could only hope he would retain the ability to see himself as he was.
He ran into the night, even though he was tired from the excessiveness of the run. He
was very motivated, though he could not run forever, even if he could not feel the effects. He had
to take several long rests throughout the day because he needed food with all of the energy that
he expended. He had found mushrooms and berries along the path he chose to run, and he knew
the plants that were edible and able to be sustaining. They also could not be poisonous. If he got
poisoned now, he would not be there to save the land, and that would be a little humiliating.
Andy found that he was more productive in his thoughts when he took time to rest for a
while, though he also had many poems come to his mind. He remembered in all of the
commotion that the gem had been put into one of their bags, but he did not know which. He
hoped that the others had kept their horses and that they still had the gem. If they lost the gem,
what would they do to remember the memories? It would also be a little embarrassing if they
lost one of the most useful things that they had gained almost as soon as they had got it.
Andy thought that there was a little hope of finding the gem, but then he remembered
what it was like to reach the gem. It was like an independent mind all on its own. If he could
reach the gem, he could reach it without having to hold it, as long as someone else did not get it.
One thing he was afraid of was that someone else would get it, possibly whatever form
the fire took. He then thought about all of the factors that were included, and then he decided
that it would be alright because the enemy likely did not know about the gem. He would have to
stumble upon it, though he knew that that also was unlikely. He could rely fairly heavily on the
assumption that the enemy would not find it.
Andy was eager to get to his friends, but he was also eager to see what form the enemy
took, for if it took the form of something directly contradictory to his power, it would be a real
challenge. All Andy could do was hope that that was not the case, but the fires had all taken the
form of something fairly related to their region, so he did not have to worry about something
stopping his thought process again as the Wincher Wales had attempted to do. The Wincher
Wales had actually provided a great opportunity for learning, as he saw it.
When Andy did not find his friends, he started trying to pay more attention to the
ground, to see if there were any tracks, or some trace of them. He knew that they were
somewhere in the general direction he was going, but he also knew he could be in the totally
opposite mountain range than he needed to be in.Even though that was a drastic measure, it
seemed like he was going in the wrong direction, almost backwards. He knew that he was going
the right way, but his worry clouded his thoughts.
Andy thought for many hours about the decision he was about to make, and he thought
that it was the right one, but he also still worried, which only clouded his thoughts further.
On the third day of running, Andy was able to run a far distance, as well as find the
tracks of horses. He hoped that they were of Nia and Liam’s horses, though he could not be sure.
He knew that not many other people would be in the forest, but he also knew that he could be
mistaking them for some servant of the fire.
When the reasons came together, Andy was relieved and more content that the chances
were low for that as well. He followed the tracks far into the mountains, and then, he found
them, but how he found them, he could never forget. Andy saw that they were with a group of
goblins. He doubted that they were the same ones that they had before entering the desert
kingdom. Andy was able to do more than he expected, though he could also see a way that he
could get through their defenses if everything went right.
There were not as many goblins in this group as there were in the other one that they
had encountered, but they still had more than fifty of the warriors. Andy had heard stories about
goblins, and he knew that the goblins were stronger in the night when the sun did not show its
warm face.
The goblins were able to capture them, but Andy did not know if they would fight
against a force as big as the goblins. They were outnumbered twenty-five to one, but they knew
that they could defeat the goblins with their minds. The goblins would be weaker, and with
Andy’s new power, he knew that he could easily take twenty of the weak goblins. He also knew
that he was more humble than before, he knew himself better than he had before, so he was able
to gain better control.
Andy was able to get a view over a small dip that the goblins were just over. The goblins
were moving with Andy and Nia, but they seemed to be nonchalant about everything that could
happen in the world. Andy decided he would get a view from down lower, so he climbed down
the small incline that separated them. He climbed through the trees, but he knew that he would
be safe, for the goblins were still about one hundred yards away.
When he got down to the bottom, he saw more clearly the numbers that the goblins were
trying to portray. He also saw that the goblins moved far slower in the daylight. He thought that
the goblins looked puny. He knew not what changed his perspective on the world so, but he
knew that it was better than worrying. Andy had to keep the perspective that he had, but he also
knew that he should not be overconfident, just enough.
Andy got to an area where he was able to see the side of the goblins' small forces well.
He also noticed that Nia and Liam were still on their horses, so it gave him hope for the gem. He
went to the side of the goblins, back off into the trees so he could assail their minds without
them having a clue of what was attacking them. He knew that he would be able to do so, for
their wits were already muddled. He knew that the goblins would panic and maybe start to run,
but he thought that he would catch them unawares. He had so many advantages that would help
him in his goal.
Andy began his attack, it was not aggressive, rather, it was a subtle thing. He changed
the way they thought and put into their minds the things he wanted them to think. He thought
that it was working, so he continued. He also kept on succeeding at getting into the minds of his
prey. Andy made them think that they wanted to sit down since they were already tired, they
probably already had the desire, but it was Andy who put it into their minds to slump and fall to
the floor.
He continued to do it until all of the goblins were slumped or fallen. He looked at the
faces of Nia and Liam, seeing the obvious wonder why the goblins had fallen. Andy had a slight
smile upon his face that only grew larger as the effects of his efforts continued to happen in
front of him.
He emerged from the trees, just as the horses that Liam and Nia were on moved forward.
Andy did not know if it was the horses that did it, or if they decided to move while they had the
chance. Andy knew that he would have to use his mind on the horses to calm them down, but he
did not know if he could calm them enough. Well, I can only try.
Andy reached for the horses' minds, and to his surprise, he was successful, though Liam
and Nia were a little afraid of what had stopped their horses. They probably think that the goblins
somehow stopped them.
He ran up to the horses while they were still stopped and hopped in front of the horses
to surprise Liam and Nia. They jumped a little, but they did not run. The horses were not
shocked because they had been calmed down by the power of Andy’s mind. Andy had been able
to calm the horses well, and he was grateful for the ability that he had.
When they spoke again, it was Nia who got off her horse to hug him. He wanted to do
the same, though he was not connected to Liam as much as he was with his sister. Andy was not
tired any longer, and the soreness in his legs seemed to fade as they hugged.
“How did you find us?” Liam asked while Nia and Andy were still embraced. Andy
Answered as they released
“I did not know where you were, but I decided it would be best if I saw where I was
going first, then got to going in the right direction. I had to climb a mountain to do so, but I
eventually found you. I was the one that told the goblins to slump and they were already
weakened by the sun, so it was easy for me to do so.” was Andy’s reply.
“Do you still have the gem that we got from the dragon?” Andy asked.
“Yes, I know why you ask, it has the memory you stored inside of it that is important for
our mission?”
“Yes, without it, we would be losing one of the most precious things that has been given
to us very kindly by a dragon. If we lost this, we also would be untrustworthy. One other thing,
did you both feel disoriented when that bear attacked?”
“Yes, it was as if time had frozen around us and we did not know what we were doing or
why we were doing it.”
“Yes, we really need to get moving though, so let us be off, and let us ride fast to find our
enemy before he strikes.”
Without another word, they got on the two hoses that they still had and Andy checked in
the saddle for the gem, just to make sure that the gem was still there. He knew that he was able
to keep it safe if it was with him, so he moved it to his bags. He would be able to protect it well
enough, but he was perhaps too humble about how he might not be able to.
The group wrote through the day and they rode in the same direction that they were
going before. They were able to travel a fair distance that day, and they could feel the power of
something drawing near, as if something was about to break and release a flood upon them.
Andy remained cautious of the feeling, and he would not let himself be taken by the
feeling and the pressure of it. Andy got to see as the mountains were highlighted again with the
sun setting and they once again appeared as silver. Andy thought of a good name for the area if
it did not already have one, The Silver Mountains.
19: Written in the Sky
As they settled in a small area partly secluded by the trees, but still visible to the path
they were traveling, they hustled about the fire with energy. It seemed that the feeling had made
all of them anxious of what the enemy would be as they traveled through the mountains. Andy
had the feeling, but he knew not to fret about it, forge was calm that he was around his friends,
he had people who he could trust. Andy knew that they would be alright.
He had gotten his bed set up, and they had picked a perfect spot to camp. They were
comfortable building a fire that night, and they all sat talking merrily around the fire. They were
happy, and they laughed a lot that night. For Andy, laughing felt very good, for he had not
laughed in a long time. He had a good time with his friends around the fire that night as they
were sitting there. They talked about many things that they were able to do and the things that
they had done. They also talked about all of the things that could possibly happen to them. The
most talked about was the jokes though, they were also the most enjoyed.
Andy laid back on his blankets, absorbing the heat of the fire, as well as looking up into
the twilight glow that covered the sky. He enjoyed nature, there was so much to enjoy about it.
The stars, the trees, the sounds, and the things that you could see shaking and running about
because of the weather. Andy thought that he could sit in nature forever and not grow tired of it.
As he stared up into the darkening sky, he was able to see the stars winking into
existence, and they only grew brighter as the night pushed on. Andy thought that he could stare
at the stars alone forever and still learn new things about it every night. He stared at the stars so
closely that night as silence flowed between them, and he thought he saw something in the sky
that was clearer than anything.
He looked at the stars closer, and he was able to make out the details of them, their color,
their size, brightness, and the way they flickered. Andy saw some of them flicker and some of
them change their color, but all of them got brighter with the night.
Andy looked for hours at the sky while the others fell asleep and the light of the fire
dimmed. He looked until his eyes drooped. He looked because he thought he saw something
there, something that told him something.
When the world seems thoughtless,
When your mind is helpless,
When you forget the greatness
Remember the foulness
Find the joy
You must learn
You must churn
You must ponder,
Not wander
If you worry
If you sleep
You lose the silent sign of hope
The words faded from Andy’s memory as he dreamed, though he could not let them fade.
The words seemed to incorporate themselves into the dreams, but they also made them very
confusing. Andy wondered if he was just seeing something in the sky, or if the stars actually
were in the way that he saw them. He had a hope that they were before he woke. He knew that
they would be fine to have the words fade, but he tried to remember as much as he could in his
dreams.
Andy wondered if there was something in the sky before, or if someone had put it there
for him to see. He always wondered if the stars would ever change, but sometimes, a change
could be bigger than you wanted it to be. Hoping for things too ambitiously could hinder the
cause more than they could forward it. Andy had thought about the concept long and hard,
which was his conclusion.
Andy thought that he could figure out much by just sitting by a fire out in the middle of
nowhere. There was so much to learn about everything, Andy wondered if it was even possible
to know all that there was in the universe, or even in the world.
In the morning, Andy woke before everyone else, and he went to collect something that
would be useful for them such as berries, which they would enjoy, but they also knew that they
would have to have more to sustain them, so he took some of the food that was in the saddlebags
to cook it. Even in the saddle bags, they would need more food to get them to the place that they
needed to go.
Andy thought that they would have to leave early in the morning to gain the ground that
they wanted, but he knew that they would have to be riding hard on the horses to do so. Andy
got some bread, and surprisingly, some of the cheese was still good. He made simple meals for
the group so they could enjoy it when they woke up. It was not soon after he thought that they
woke, and they could smell the grilled cheese that he was making. They still had their cooking
supplies, but they only seemed to have a pot and pan, though they sufficed.
Andy cooked his simple, tasty meal quickly and remembered to serve the others first
before taking the food himself. They only needed three, but as they were eating, Andy wondered
if the falcon was still following them, or if they had lost him in the confusion. Andy thought that
the bird would not get lost as easily as they would, but he did worry about the bird, even though
he was very wise.
“Do you know where Eradris is? Has he followed you here?” Andy asked when the others
joined him to prepare the horses that were tied to a tree.
“Ah, he is always up there, he just does not say anything unless it is necessary. He seems
to think that we can do things on our own without his help.” came Liam's reply, for he was
preparing the horses too. Andy went to extreme measures to think about the bird, for after the
incident where they had found out about his true identity, they were more cautious of him, as
well as a little bit grateful. They knew that without his help, they would not have been able to
beat the Wincher Wales, but he did know that they would have been decimated if he was not
there.
Andy thought that they were getting close to finding the shadow, but he could not be
sure. He was too entranced in the stars. The stars he had seen the night before were beautiful
and Andy remembered all that he had thought that was inspired by the stars. The stars were so
beautiful, and he wanted to look more.
They left soon after their small conversation was over, and Andy thought that the feeling
they had felt before increased and got stronger. Andy thought that they would have to ride
quickly if they were going to get to where they were going before something happened, though
he did not bother to look up. Andy thought that the fire would be on the ground, though he did
not know. Andy would have to be more aware in the future.
The next night when they looked at the stars, they did not as long as they had when Andy
stared at them last, but they seemed to lose the writing that was in the sky. Andy loved the stars
anyway, but he could only stare at them for so long, for he needed rest, he needed strength.
He had a strange dream that made no sense, but he could make sense of it as he slept
that night. He could see in the eyes of someone great, he could perceive and observe. Many
things were there, but people just do not see them, they do not observe. Andy learned then how
he could observe even in the strangest circumstances, even if it was in a dream.
Andy thought that the night that he had seen the writing was almost insignificant,
though he did not know why. Was it just his mind changing on its own, or was something
changing it.
Something terrible came to Andy’s mind, what if he had been manipulated by something
else while he was ignorant that others could do so. What if people had gotten to him back when
they were still content in their town. It was not something Andy wanted to accept, though he
knew he might have to do so.
Andy thought that they would make it far, but then he felt a dramatic increase in the
feeling he had had before, it seemed as if there was some deep festering in the region. They
decided to keep going in the same direction, for it felt that the enemy would be closer to the
feeling of unease. The enemy always was at the climax of the unease, but it also had some twist
that it used.
Then it hit Andy, he looked up. There was a storm where you could see the clouds
actively flowing across the land. The clouds seemed a little too low, and they covered the tops of
the mountains. Andy then knew why they had been ignorant of the sky, but they had also been
ignorant of the festering clouds. The clouds were dark, and it seemed to sink as if it wanted to
pick them up and blow them away.
Andy told the others of the festering clouds that they all had ignored for too long. Andy
knew that the clouds were not just a storm, it was too swift and rapid for the time of year. Andy
almost was afraid of the clouds, but he could not escape their blanketing view of the land. They
obscured all of the land, though it was still a small part of the world, and it still seemed so big.
They were also a little afraid of the clouds, and they did not know why they had not seen
the clouds before. Andy looked at the clouds over and over, and as they traveled through the
land, they seemed to get closer and sink to them. He was uneasy, but he knew to keep moving.
He knew that he would be caught by a storm or something bad if they did not ride faster. They
then continued at a gallop. When they started to do that, snowflakes started to touch their face,
and it was not harsh, but it remained calm and slow falling.
They continued, but they were soon greeted by another problem, a breeze blew through,
and with it, the cold. Andy checked in the bags of the saddle for something that would help keep
them warm. Andy looked around at them and saw that Liam had his leather jacket on, which
Andy had seen before, though he did not really pay it any heed. He found four jackets in the bag
which he did not know that they had packed extra, though he was grateful. He gave one to Nia
and left one out for himself. He put the other two back in the bag, and put the other one on
while Nia put hers on.
It broke the cold wind that had arrived, but it was still cold, for the wind penetrated the
jacket. For the most part, they were warm enough, but Andy's ears were very cold still. He
wrapped his jacket around himself, though Andy knew it might make things worse, for the
jacket would be closer to himself and easier for the wind to penetrate.
Though the wind was cold, nature, or not, the storm wanted to throw something totally
out of the ordinary. Andy knew after he saw it that the storm was not just a small thing that was
born from the effects of nature. He knew that the storm was going to give something to them,
and this had to be it. A tornado touched down in the middle of the small valley that they were in.
Andy happened to see this as he turned around to look at what was behind him. He saw
the horrendous storm as he went to check his surroundings. The storm started to chase them
through the valley as a wild animal, though it was far more destructive, and it was faster than
anything Andy had ever seen. The tornado tore through the land, and it was the sound that
reached them then. The sound was echoing through the land, but they still had to run.
“Go!” Andy shouted to the others. They spurred their horses forward as they saw the
tornado emerge from the trees bringing with it a few, and then bring the grass out of the
ground. They could feel the rage of the storm, and it felt as if they would be caught. They might
not be able to escape this one. The horrendous snow and storm reminded Andy of the time that
he had been in his town out in the trees with Nia, and they had gotten trapped in the hut by the
snow, though the wind was stronger. Andy felt the same way now, alone, as if the storm sucked
the others' thoughts away.
The storm even seemed to suck the thought out of his own mind. He did not like that he
was getting beat again, but what could they do to stop the storm. Andy could not mistake it for
an illusion, he could feel the power of the storm.
Despite the speed of their horses, the storm gained ground, which was not surprising,
but certainly terrifying. Andy wondered if the fire in this region was powerful enough to
summon something so big, then another thought came to him, what if the storm was the fire? If
the storm had taken the form of air and wind, how could they defeat something that resided all
around them?
They ran for a while before they knew that the storm would catch them. It was very close
now, and they could not escape it. Andy looked for escape even though he did not expect to find
any. He looked around, but his thoughts were dampened and slow. Andy suddenly saw
something when looking that he had not expected. There was a cave off to the side that showed
them the way to safety. Andy pointed it out to the group and they tried to get to it before the
storm caught them. They could feel the storm, it threatened to sweep them off the ground, and
they thought they would, then they got into the cave.
They looked out of the cave while trying to go as far back from it as they could. As they
did, they thought they saw the storm pass the cave. It appeared as if the storm had a face, and it
seemed to smile evilly and with great amusement. Andy wondered if the storm actually had a
face, or if he was just being tricked again, as he had before.
They were safe, and they could hear each other taking deep breaths. It was cold still but
they were safe from the storm. If the fire had taken the form of the wind or the air, what would
they do to defeat it? What countered the wind. They would have to have the lack of air, or the
opposite of it. They could not think of what it would be that could put the enemy down.
Andy was a little tired, though they knew they would need to keep going. Andy feared
that another storm might be conjured by the fire if they went out again, but he knew it was
necessary that they continued. Then Andy thought that they could counter the wind only if they
were there to stop it.
Together, they decided that they would stay there, but they needed to explore the cave
just to make sure nothing was there. It went farther into the mountain than Andy expected, and
Andy knew that they would need light to go into the cave.
Outside, the snow was now falling calmly again, but it had increased in its size. The
snow was now about the size that it had been when they were in the mountains in the tundra. It
was as big as their palm, and they were able to watch as their world turned white. The snow was
big enough that it covered the ground within a matter of a few minutes.
They decided that they probably would not be able to travel in the cold, and they did not
want to risk being caught by a tornado with a mind. They would surely be in trouble if they got
caught by the tornado. They sat in the cave, wondering and thinking about what they were to do
now that they were at a stop again. Andy was annoyed, though he did not know why, perhaps he
wanted to get going quickly. Andy was annoyed, but he thought that he would have to stay there
for a while, so he decided to make a fire.
Andy made the fire, and it helped to cut the cold’s stinging touch. Andy thought that the
cold could make a fire easily in all of the conditions of the weather, but he knew that he would
need to get his rest. It would help them to rest and regain their strength, but he did not know if
they would have rest, for the wind a=outside got louder. It got loud enough that Andy had to
cover his ears because something out in the wind was whistling.
They figure they would make camp inside the cave, for the storm did not show any signs
of stopping. The group was calm, and the wind calmed too, but they still got sudden bursts of
wind that blew into their cave. The snow did not get into the cave, but it was also calming down,
the flakes were not as big.
Outside, it was darkening quickly, for the clouds added extra darkness to the sky and
dampened the light that could get through. Andy thought that something like that would be
hard to endure, the cold, but it turned out, his fire got warm enough for him to stay comfortable.
Andy was capable of enduring the cold though he did not like it, and he actually liked the snow
when it came softly. When he could look out over the land and see the snow softly falling, then
in the morning see it gleaming on the mountains of silver ice, and see the thoughts written in
the sky with the passing storm.
20: The Storm Returns
When Andy woke up in the morning, he woke up late. The others were already up and
they tended to the horses, who stood nearer to the door that they did. Andy got up so he could
get something to eat out of the saddlebags.
He walked over to the horses and retrieved some bread that he hungrily gobbled down.
When he finished the bread, Andy was not quite full, but he was very content. He had not had
something to eat that was cooked for a few days, and he was grateful for the simple thing. Sat
down, and no one said anything, they only made the noises of eating or tending to the animals.
Outside, the ground was covered with a few feet of snow, and the snow was very wet.
They had plenty of water that they could warm if they needed to, but they were fine for the
moment. They had food, they were content, but they did need to move, so they all anxiously
awaited the sun, that was stuck behind a cloud to melt the snow enough.
The sun finally came out from behind the clouds and shed its rays upon the snow, which
was blinding and gave Andy a greenish reddish dot in the middle of his vision for the next few
minutes. Andy was ready to go, but he knew that they could not travel well in the snow. It would
be more harmful to travel in the snow, but he would still be able to travel.
Just then, Eradris flew into the cave and gave a report to them. Andy was able to get
working well with him and communicate well with the bird.
Andy, you are not safe here, you need to move. I know it will be difficult in the snow, but it is
more dangerous here. Andy, tell the others quickly, there is another tornado that is made of fire, and it is
the same size. Leave quickly.
Andy did as the bird said, and wondered why the bird was so worried, but then he
convinced himself of the bird’s wisdom and did as it said. If the tornado got to them, what could
go wrong when they were in the cave. They would be fine in the cave, though Andy did not have
the view that the bird did.
The others hustled to get their provisions packed into the bags, and they were able to get
out into the snow easily. There were trees around them, which were not as covered with snow
below them, so they traveled better under their boughs. The trees hung low with the weight of
the snow upon them, but they were not exempt from obstacles. The fire continued to come
closer, though they did not know where it was. They had to rely on Eradris’s view for their
information.
The group traveled for a while, and they were able to find a thicker forested area that the
boughs did not grow as low, so they were able to speed their pace. When they were making good
ground, they had to take advantage of the gap so they could escape the fire.
They started to feel a heat upon their backs, and when they turned to see the cause, they
saw the tornado after them once more. They continued, and they were able to go fast, but a
tornado was still faster than them, especially when it was made of fire and they were slowed by
the snow.
They continued, despite the challenge of the fire, and the daunting pressure it put upon
them, and they did not even know where they were to go. Andy decided to ask Eradris where
they would be going for the next few minutes, Where are we going, or are we just going to our death?
We no longer have a cave to stay in and we dare to go back the way we came.
Eradris only answered with a small peck in his hand and then told him, There is something
ahead.
They continued to run, and they were lucky with the fact that they started to see less and
less snow on the ground, they were even coming out of the forested area, and the snow still
lessened. It was good that they had this happen, it might be their only chance. If they could buy

Andy thought that this fire was clever, and that it had much planning behind its

Andy did not want to imagine it. They emerged totally out of the trees and into a

Andy thought that they might not make it to the safe area that Eradris talked about,

Andy was curious about it, though he did not ask Eradris, or he would be questioned and

They continued to run to the horses, though they could not be as fast as the tornado. Suddenly,

They were able to get into the valley safely, for the tornado seemed to be pushing past

At the other side of the wall, there was a big hole, though this hole was filled with the

reached the bottom. The valley also changed around them, the mountains around them were

valley was breathtaking. When they were staring at the tree, they saw something disturbing it

The tree cast a shadow upon the land, and the rivers flowed with the sun shimmering on

were fast, but they were also almost like something they had seen before. The group that came

Andy did not think that the Oakenash would be in the mountains, but he supposed they

mean it was exempt from being able to destroy.
The Oakenash’s attention was turned toward the tornado as it spotted their position and

The Oakenash were able to maneuver about the tornado, and with how light they were,

Something started to happen after the spell was uttered. The effects of the spell took

The spell was confusing, though the world did not seem to be affected. When they had

it, ah, water.
The Oakenash that were in the midst of the tornado were leading the waterfalls that

This time, they did not defeat the fire, but they certainly did get it to the Oakenash. They

that is, if the regions were still free.
It struck Andy, just how long they had been on the quest, and they were there together
